const SAMPLE_STMT=[{"label": "Collection A/c (PDF)", "name": "Sample_Bank_Statement_1_Collection_Account.pdf", "kind": "pdf", "b64": "JVBERi0xLjQKJZOMi54gUmVwb3 ... [long embedded data omitted, 12329 characters]
<script>/* ================= MILES2COLLECT CORE LOGIC ================= */
const MON = ['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
const DAY = 86400000;
const utc = (y, m, d) => new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, d));
function toDate(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return null;
  if (v instanceof Date) return isNaN(v) ? null : utc(v.getFullYear(), v.getMonth(), v.getDate());
  if (typeof v === 'number' && v > 20000 && v < 80000) return new Date(Math.round((v - 25569) * DAY));
  const s = String(v).trim();
  let m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-\/. ](\d{1,2})[-\/. ](\d{2,4})$/);
  if (m) { let y = +m[3]; if (y < 100) y += 2000; return utc(y, +m[2] - 1, +m[1]); }
  m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-\/. ]([A-Za-z]{3})[a-z]*[-\/. ,]*(\d{2,4})$/);
  if (m) { const mi = MON.findIndex(x => x.toLowerCase() === m[2].toLowerCase()); let y = +m[3]; if (y < 100) y += 2000; if (mi >= 0) return utc(y, mi, +m[1]); }
  m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
  if (m) return utc(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3]);
  return null;
}
const fmtDate = d => d ? String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + MON[d.getUTCMonth()] + '-' + d.getUTCFullYear() : '';
const addDays = (d, n) => new Date(d.getTime() + n * DAY);
const daysBetween = (a, b) => Math.round((b - a) / DAY);
const num = v => { if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return 0; const n = typeof v === 'number' ? v : parseFloat(String(v).replace(/[₹,\s]/g, '')); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; };
const inr = n => { n = Math.round(num(n)); const neg = n < 0; let s = String(Math.abs(n)); if (s.length > 3) { const last = s.slice(-3); let rest = s.slice(0, -3); rest = rest.replace(/\B(?=(\d{2})+(?!\d))/g, ','); s = rest + ',' + last; } return (neg ? '-' : '') + s; };
const rs = n => '₹ ' + inr(n);
function cr(n) { n = num(n); const a = Math.abs(n); if (a >= 1e7) return '₹ ' + (n / 1e7).toFixed(2) + ' Cr'; if (a >= 1e5) return '₹ ' + (n / 1e5).toFixed(2) + ' L'; return '₹ ' + inr(n); }
const pct1 = n => (Math.round(num(n) * 10) / 10).toFixed(1) + '%';
const normFlat = s => { const t = String(s).trim(); return /^\d+$/.test(t) ? String(parseInt(t, 10)) : t.toUpperCase(); };
function ordinal(n) { const s = ['th', 'st', 'nd', 'rd'], v = n % 100; return n + (s[(v - 20) % 10] || s[v] || s[0]); }

function amountInWords(n) {
  n = Math.round(num(n));
  if (n === 0) return 'Zero';
  const ones = ['', 'One', 'Two', 'Three', 'Four', 'Five', 'Six', 'Seven', 'Eight', 'Nine', 'Ten', 'Eleven', 'Twelve', 'Thirteen', 'Fourteen', 'Fifteen', 'Sixteen', 'Seventeen', 'Eighteen', 'Nineteen'];
  const tens = ['', '', 'Twenty', 'Thirty', 'Forty', 'Fifty', 'Sixty', 'Seventy', 'Eighty', 'Ninety'];
  const two = x => x < 20 ? ones[x] : tens[Math.floor(x / 10)] + (x % 10 ? ' ' + ones[x % 10] : '');
  const three = x => (x >= 100 ? ones[Math.floor(x / 100)] + ' Hundred' + (x % 100 ? ' ' : '') : '') + (x % 100 ? two(x % 100) : '');
  let out = [];
  const cr_ = Math.floor(n / 1e7); n %= 1e7;
  const lk = Math.floor(n / 1e5); n %= 1e5;
  const th = Math.floor(n / 1e3); n %= 1e3;
  if (cr_) out.push(three(cr_) + ' Crore');
  if (lk) out.push(two(lk) + ' Lakh');
  if (th) out.push(two(th) + ' Thousand');
  if (n) out.push(three(n));
  return out.join(' ');
}

/* ---------------- Excel parsing ---------------- */
function parseWorkbook(XLSX, buf) {
  const wb = XLSX.read(buf, { type: 'array', cellDates: false });
  const out = { sales: [], units: [], customers: [], schedule: [], info: {}, cost: [], found: [] };
  const sheetRows = sn => XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets[sn], { header: 1, defval: null, raw: true });
  const has = (r, t) => r && r.some(c => c !== null && String(c).trim().toLowerCase() === t.toLowerCase());
  for (const sn of wb.SheetNames) {
    const rows = sheetRows(sn);
    // ---- sales summary
    let hi = rows.findIndex(r => has(r, 'Flat No') && has(r, 'Flat Value'));
    if (hi >= 0 && !out.sales.length) {
      const H = rows[hi].map(c => c === null ? '' : String(c).trim());
      const nth = (name, k) => { let n = 0; for (let i = 0; i < H.length; i++) if (H[i].toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase()) { if (n === k) return i; n++; } return -1; };
      const starts = (p) => H.findIndex(h => h.toLowerCase().startsWith(p));
      const fv = nth('Flat Value', 0);
      const col = { flat: nth('Flat No', 0), cust: nth('Customer Name', 0), contact: starts('customer contact'),
        axisColl: nth('Axis Collection', 0), axis30: nth('Axis-30%', 0), otherBank: nth('Other Bank', 0), odaGst: nth('BOI/Axis ODA/c for GST', 0),
        chqRetCr: nth('Cheque Return', 0), tds: nth('TDS', 0), sdRegCr: nth('Stamp Duty+Reg', 0), salesJv: nth('Sales JV', 0), chqBounced: starts('chq bounced'),
        chqRetDr: nth('Cheque Return', 1), gstDr: nth('GST', 0), sdRegDr: nth('Stamp Duty+Reg', 1),
        av: fv, recv: fv + 1, avBal: fv + 2, pct: fv + 3, gst: fv + 4, gstRecd: fv + 5, gstBal: fv + 6, sd: fv + 7, sdRecd: fv + 8, sdBal: fv + 9 };
      for (let r = hi + 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        const row = rows[r]; if (!row) continue;
        const f = row[col.flat];
        if (f === null || f === undefined) continue;
        const fs = String(f).trim();
        if (!/^\d{3,4}$/.test(fs)) { if (/^sale$/i.test(fs) || /^total/i.test(fs)) break; continue; }
        const g = k => col[k] >= 0 ? num(row[col[k]]) : 0;
        out.sales.push({ label: fs, norm: normFlat(fs), customer: row[col.cust] ? String(row[col.cust]).trim() : '', contact: row[col.contact] || '',
          av: g('av'), recv: g('recv'), avBal: g('avBal'), pct: g('pct'), gst: g('gst'), gstRecd: g('gstRecd'), gstBal: g('gstBal'),
          sd: g('sd'), sdRecd: g('sdRecd'), sdBal: g('sdBal'),
          bounced: g('chqBounced') + g('chqRetDr'),
          cr: { axisColl: g('axisColl'), axis30: g('axis30'), otherBank: g('otherBank'), odaGst: g('odaGst'), tds: g('tds'), sdReg: g('sdRegCr') },
          dr: { salesJv: g('salesJv'), chqBounced: g('chqBounced') } });
      }
      out.found.push('Sales ledger (' + sn + ')');
      continue;
    }
    // ---- units
    hi = rows.findIndex(r => has(r, 'Flat No') && (has(r, 'Area Sq Ft') || has(r, 'Area')));
    if (hi >= 0 && !out.units.length) {
      for (let r = hi + 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        const row = rows[r]; if (!row || row[0] === null) continue;
        const f = String(row[0]).trim();
        if (/^total/i.test(f)) break;
        if (!/^\d{3,4}$/.test(f)) continue;
        const a = typeof row[1] === 'number' ? row[1] : parseFloat(row[1]);
        const n = parseInt(f, 10);
        out.units.push({ no: f, norm: normFlat(f), area: isNaN(a) ? 0 : a, saleable: !isNaN(a) && a > 0, note: isNaN(a) ? String(row[1] || '') : '', floor: Math.floor(n / 100) });
      }
      out.found.push('Unit list (' + sn + ')');
      continue;
    }
    // ---- customer master
    hi = rows.findIndex(r => has(r, 'Flat No') && has(r, 'Email'));
    if (hi >= 0 && !out.customers.length) {
      const H = rows[hi].map(c => c === null ? '' : String(c).trim().toLowerCase());
      const ix = p => H.findIndex(h => h.startsWith(p));
      for (let r = hi + 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        const row = rows[r]; if (!row || row[0] === null) continue;
        const f = String(row[0]).trim(); if (!/^\d{3,4}$/.test(f)) continue;
        out.customers.push({ norm: normFlat(f), name: row[ix('customer name')] || '', email: row[ix('email')] || '', mobile: row[ix('mobile')] || '', address: row[ix('correspondence')] || '',
          state: String(ix('state') >= 0 ? (row[ix('state')] || '') : '').trim(), agreementDate: toDate(row[ix('agreement date')]), registered: String(row[ix('agreement registered')] || 'N').trim().toUpperCase().startsWith('Y'), regNo: row[ix('registration no')] || '', plan: row[ix('payment plan')] || '', gstin:ix('recipient gstin')>=0?String(row[ix('recipient gstin')]||''):'', loanBank: row[ix('home loan')] || '', van: ix('virtual')>=0 ? String(row[ix('virtual')]||'').trim() : '' });
      }
      out.found.push('Customer master');
      continue;
    }
    // ---- schedule
    hi = rows.findIndex(r => r && r.some(c => c !== null && /^milestone/i.test(String(c).trim())));
    if (hi >= 0 && !out.schedule.length) {
      const H = rows[hi].map(c => c === null ? '' : String(c).trim().toLowerCase());
      const ix = p => H.findIndex(h => h.startsWith(p));
      let cum = 0;
      for (let r = hi + 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        const row = rows[r]; if (!row || row[ix('milestone')] === null || row[ix('milestone')] === undefined) continue;
        let p = num(row[ix('%')]); if (p > 1) p = p / 100;
        cum += p;
        out.schedule.push({ n: out.schedule.length + 1, name: String(row[ix('milestone')]).trim(), pct: p, achieved: String(row[ix('status')] || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('ach'), date: toDate(row[ix('achieved date')]),
          demandRaised: ix('demand raised') >= 0 ? String(row[ix('demand raised')] || '').trim().toUpperCase().startsWith('Y') : true });
      }
      out.found.push('Company payment schedule');
      continue;
    }
    // ---- project info
    if (rows.length && has(rows[0], 'Field') && has(rows[0], 'Value')) {
      rows.slice(1).forEach(r => { if (r && r[0] && r[1] !== null && r[1] !== undefined) out.info[String(r[0]).trim()] = r[1]; });
      out.found.push('Project info');
      continue;
    }
    // ---- cost
    hi = rows.findIndex(r => has(r, 'Cost Head'));
    if (hi >= 0 && !out.cost.length) {
      for (let r = hi + 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        const row = rows[r]; if (!row || !row[0] || typeof row[1] !== 'number') continue;
        out.cost.push({ head: String(row[0]).trim(), budget: num(row[1]), incurred: num(row[2]) });
      }
      out.found.push('Project cost');
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/* ---------------- State ---------------- */
function wipeState(){Object.keys(S).forEach(k=>delete S[k]);Object.assign(S,newState());}
function newState() {
  return { info: {}, units: [], unitMap: new Map(), sales: new Map(), customers: new Map(), schedule: [], custSchedules: new Map(),
    cost: [], txns: [], files: [], demands: [], asOfBase: utc(2026, 9, 23), loaded: false };
}
function infoGet(S, key, def) { const v = S.info[key]; return (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') ? def : v; }
function applyParsed(S, P) {
  if (P.units.length) { S.units = P.units; S.unitMap = new Map(P.units.map(u => [u.norm, u])); }
  if (P.sales.length) S.sales = new Map(P.sales.map(s => [s.norm, s]));
  if (P.customers.length) S.customers = new Map(P.customers.map(c => [c.norm, c]));
  if (P.schedule.length) { S.schedule = P.schedule; S.custSchedules = new Map(); }
  if (P.cost.length) S.cost = P.cost;
  if (Object.keys(P.info).length) S.info = Object.assign({}, S.info, P.info);
  const d = toDate(S.info['Sales Data As On']); if (d) S.asOfBase = d;
  S.loaded = true;
}
function cfg(S) {
  return { dueDays: num(infoGet(S, 'Demand Payable Within (days)', 15)), rate: num(infoGet(S, 'Interest Rate % p.a.', 12)),
    tol: num(infoGet(S, 'Shortfall Tolerance (Rs)', 5000)), gstRate: num(infoGet(S, 'GST Rate on Sale', 0.05)) };
}
function asOfDate(S) {
  let d = S.asOfBase;
  S.txns.forEach(t => { if (t.date && t.date > d) d = t.date; });
  return d;
}

/* ---------------- Bank statements ---------------- */
function matchFlat(S, narr) {
  const n = String(narr).toUpperCase();
  let m = n.match(/(?:FLAT|FLT|UNIT|APT|F\/NO|FL)[\s\-:\/#.]*(?:NO\.?)?[\s\-:]*0*(\d{3,4})\b/);
  if (m && S.sales.has(normFlat(m[1]))) return normFlat(m[1]);
  m = n.match(/\b0(\d{3})\b/);
  if (m && S.sales.has(normFlat(m[1]))) return normFlat(m[1]);
  return null;
}
const COSTMAP = [
  [/ARCHITECT|CONSULT|PROF\w* FEE|AUDIT|LEGAL|ADVOCATE|STRUCTURAL/, 'Professional fees'],
  [/MUNICIPAL|MCGM|PLAN APPROVAL|NOC|PREMIUM|APPROVAL/, 'Approvals & statutory charges'],
  [/CONSTRUCTION|CONTRACTOR|RA BILL|ELECTRICAL|LIFT|PLUMB|STEEL|CEMENT|TRADERS|MATERIAL|RMC|SAND|TILES|PAINT/, 'Construction (civil, MEP, finishing)'],
  [/BROKERAGE|MARKETING|ADVERT|HOARDING/, 'Marketing & brokerage'],
  [/SALARY|RENT|ADMIN|OFFICE|STATIONERY/, 'Admin & overheads'],
  [/INTEREST|LOAN|EMI/, 'Finance cost']
];
function classifyTxn(S, t) {
  const n = String(t.narr).toUpperCase();
  const mb = matchFlatBy(S, n), flat = mb.flat;
  if (t.cr > 0) {
    if (/TRF FROM|SWEEP|INTER.?A\/?C|OWN A\/C/.test(n)) return { kind: 'transfer_in' };
    if (/INT(EREST)? ?(PD|PAID|CREDIT)|CREDIT INTEREST/.test(n)) return { kind: 'bank_interest' };
    if(flat && /M2C-\d{3,4}-M\d+/i.test(n) && S.demands.some(d=>d.flat===flat && n.includes(d.ref))) return {kind:'demand_payment',flat,by:mb.by+' + demand ref'};
    if(flat){const dm=openDemandByAmount(S,flat,t.cr);if(dm)return {kind:'demand_payment',flat,by:mb.by+' + amount',demRef:dm.ref};}
    if (flat) return { kind: /STAMP|REG(ISTRATION)? ?CHG|GRAS/.test(n) ? 'stamp' : /\bGST\b/.test(n) ? 'gst' : 'instalment', flat, by: mb.by };
    return { kind: 'unmatched' };
  }
  if (/TRF TO.*RERA|RERA.*SWEEP/.test(n)) return { kind: 'transfer_rera' };
  if (/TRF TO|SWEEP/.test(n)) return { kind: 'transfer_free' };
  if (/CHQ RETURN|CHEQUE RETURN|CHQ RTN|INWARD RETURN/.test(n) && !/CHARGE|CHG/.test(n)) return { kind: 'chq_return', flat };
  if (/CHARGES|CHG|SMS/.test(n)) return { kind: 'bank_charges', flat };
  if (/STAMP DUTY|ESBTR|GRAS/.test(n)) return { kind: 'tax_paid', cat: 'Stamp duty remitted' };
  if (/GST PAYMENT|PMT-06|GSTR/.test(n)) return { kind: 'tax_paid', cat: 'GST remitted' };
  if (/\bTDS\b|26Q|CHALLAN 281/.test(n)) return { kind: 'tax_paid', cat: 'TDS remitted' };
  for (const [rx, head] of COSTMAP) if (rx.test(n)) return { kind: 'cost', cat: head };
  return { kind: 'other_payment', cat: 'Uncategorised' };
}
function addTxns(S, txns, fileName) {
  const seen = new Set(S.txns.map(t => t.key));
  let added = 0, dup = 0;
  txns.forEach(t => {
    const key = [t.date ? t.date.getTime() : '', t.narr, t.dr, t.cr].join('|');
    if (seen.has(key)) { dup++; return; }
    seen.add(key);
    const c = classifyTxn(S, t);
    S.txns.push(Object.assign({ key, id: S.txns.length + 1, file: fileName, kind: c.kind, flat: c.flat || null, cat: c.cat || '', matchBy: c.by || '', demRef: c.demRef || '' }, t));
    added++;
  });
  if (!S.files.includes(fileName)) S.files.push(fileName);
  S.txns.sort((a, b) => (a.date - b.date) || (a.id - b.id));
  return { added, dup };
}
function parseCSVRows(rows) {
  const lc = c => (c === null || c === undefined) ? '' : String(c).trim().toLowerCase();
  let hi = rows.findIndex(r => r && r.some(c => /date/.test(lc(c))) && r.some(c => /particular|narration|description|remarks/.test(lc(c))) && r.some(c => /debit|withdrawal|\bdr\b/.test(lc(c))) && r.some(c => /credit|deposit|\bcr\b/.test(lc(c))));
  if (hi < 0) return { txns: [], error: 'Could not find a header row with Date, Particulars, Debit and Credit columns.' };
  const H = rows[hi].map(lc);
  const ix = rx => H.findIndex(h => rx.test(h));
  const cDate = ix(/date/), cNarr = ix(/particular|narration|description|remarks/), cDr = ix(/debit|withdrawal|\bdr\b/), cCr = ix(/credit|deposit|\bcr\b/), cBal = ix(/balance|\bbal\b/), cChq = ix(/chq|cheque|ref/);
  const txns = [];
  for (let r = hi + 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
    const row = rows[r]; if (!row) continue;
    const d = toDate(row[cDate]); if (!d) continue;
    const dr = num(row[cDr]), cr_ = num(row[cCr]);
    if (!dr && !cr_) continue;
    txns.push({ date: d, chq: cChq >= 0 && row[cChq] ? String(row[cChq]).trim() : '', narr: String(row[cNarr] || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(), dr, cr: cr_, bal: cBal >= 0 ? num(row[cBal]) : 0 });
  }
  return { txns };
}
/* PDF lines -> transactions.  lines = [ [ {x, w, s}, ... ] , ... ] in reading order */
function parsePdfLines(lines) {
  const isAmt = s => /^\(?-?\d[\d,]*\.\d{2}\)?(Cr|Dr|CR|DR)?$/.test(s);
  const dateRx = /^(\d{1,2}[-\/]\d{1,2}[-\/]\d{2,4}|\d{1,2}[-\/ ][A-Za-z]{3}[-\/ ]\d{2,4})$/;
  let cols = null; const txns = []; let cur = null; let prevBal = null;
  const flush = () => { if (cur) { cur.narr = cur.narrParts.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); delete cur.narrParts; txns.push(cur); cur = null; } };
  lines.forEach(items => {
    const texts = items.map(i => i.s);
    const joined = texts.join(' ');
    // header line detection
    if (/debit|withdrawal/i.test(joined) && /credit|deposit/i.test(joined) && /balance/i.test(joined) && !items.some(i => dateRx.test(i.s))) {
      cols = {};
      items.forEach(i => { const c = i.x + i.w / 2; if (/^(debit|withdrawal|dr)/i.test(i.s)) cols.dr = c; else if (/^(credit|deposit|cr)/i.test(i.s)) cols.cr = c; else if (/^balance|^bal/i.test(i.s)) cols.bal = c; });
      return;
    }
    const di = items.findIndex(i => dateRx.test(i.s));
    if (di >= 0 && di <= 2) {
      flush();
      const date = toDate(items[di].s);
      const amts = items.filter((i, k) => k > di && isAmt(i.s));
      const rest = items.filter((i, k) => k > di && !isAmt(i.s));
      let dr = 0, crd = 0, bal = 0;
      if (cols && cols.dr !== undefined && cols.cr !== undefined) {
        amts.forEach(a => { const c = a.x + a.w / 2; const cand = [['dr', cols.dr], ['cr', cols.cr], ['bal', cols.bal === undefined ? 1e9 : cols.bal]];
          cand.sort((p, q) => Math.abs(p[1] - c) - Math.abs(q[1] - c)); const v = num(a.s.replace(/[()]/g, '').replace(/(Cr|Dr)$/i, ''));
          if (cand[0][0] === 'dr') dr = v; else if (cand[0][0] === 'cr') crd = v; else bal = v; });
      } else if (amts.length) {
        bal = num(amts[amts.length - 1].s); const first = amts.length > 1 ? num(amts[0].s) : 0;
        if (first) { if (prevBal !== null && bal < prevBal) dr = first; else if (prevBal !== null) crd = first; else crd = first; }
      }
      if (bal) prevBal = bal;
      let chq = ''; const parts = rest.map(r => r.s);
      if (parts.length >= 2 && /^\d{5,9}$/.test(parts[0])) { chq = parts.shift(); }
      // drop leading serial number
      cur = { date, chq, narrParts: [parts.join(' ')], dr, cr: crd, bal };
      return;
    }
    if (cur) {
      if (/closing balance|opening balance|page \d|statement|total/i.test(joined)) { flush(); return; }
      if (items.some(i => isAmt(i.s))) { flush(); return; }
      cur.narrParts.push(joined);
    }
  });
  flush();
  return txns.filter(t => t.date && (t.dr || t.cr));
}

/* ---------------- Computation ---------------- */
function splitDemandReceipt(S,t){
 const d=S.demands.find(d=>d.flat===t.flat && (t.narr.toUpperCase().includes(d.ref)||t.demRef===d.ref));
 if(!d || !(d.amount>0))return {principal:t.cr,gst:0,sd:0,interest:0};
 const prior=S.txns.filter(q=>q.id<t.id && q.kind==='demand_payment' && q.flat===t.flat && (q.narr.toUpperCase().includes(d.ref)||q.demRef===d.ref)).reduce((a,q)=>a+q.cr,0);
 const applied=Math.max(0,Math.min(t.cr,d.amount-prior)),ratio=applied/d.amount,round=n=>Math.round(n*100)/100;
 const gst=round((d.gst||0)*ratio),sd=round((d.sd||0)*ratio),interest=round((d.interest||0)*ratio),rounding=round((d.rounding||0)*ratio);
 return {principal:round(applied-gst-sd-interest-rounding+Math.max(0,t.cr-applied)),gst,sd,interest,rounding};
}
function bankAdj(S) {
  const adj = new Map();
  const get = f => { if (!adj.has(f)) adj.set(f, { inst: 0, sd: 0, gst: 0, rounding:0, bounced: 0, items: [] }); return adj.get(f); };
  S.txns.forEach(t => {
    const f = t.flat; if (!f || !S.sales.has(f)) return;
    const a = get(f);
    if(t.kind==='demand_payment'){const split=splitDemandReceipt(S,t);a.inst+=split.principal;a.gst+=split.gst;a.sd+=split.sd;a.rounding+=(split.rounding||0);a.items.push(t);}
    else if (t.kind === 'instalment') { a.inst += t.cr; a.items.push(t); }
    else if (t.kind === 'stamp') { a.sd += t.cr; a.items.push(t); }
    else if (t.kind === 'gst') { a.gst += t.cr; a.items.push(t); }
    else if (t.kind === 'chq_return') { a.inst -= t.dr; a.bounced += t.dr; a.items.push(t); }
  });
  return adj;
}
function shortName(n) {
  const s = n.toLowerCase();
  let m;
  if (/booking/.test(s)) return 'Booking';
  if (/agreement/.test(s)) return 'Agreement';
  if (/plinth/.test(s)) return 'Plinth';
  if (/first slab/.test(s)) return '1st slab';
  if ((m = s.match(/(\d+)(?:st|nd|rd|th) slab/))) return ordinal(+m[1]) + ' slab';
  if (/top .*slab/.test(s)) return 'Top slab';
  if (/walls/.test(s)) return 'Walls & plaster';
  if (/sanitary/.test(s)) return 'Sanitary & lobbies';
  if (/external plumbing/.test(s)) return 'Elevation & plumbing';
  if (/lifts/.test(s)) return 'Lifts & electrical';
  if (/final finishing/.test(s)) return 'Final finishing';
  if (/finishing/.test(s)) return 'Finishing';
  if (/handover/.test(s)) return 'Handover';
  if (/occupancy/.test(s)) return 'OC received';
  if (/possession/.test(s)) return 'Possession';
  return n.length > 22 ? n.slice(0, 21) + '…' : n;
}
function scheduleFor(S, norm) { return S.custSchedules.get(norm) || S.schedule.map(m => m.pct); }
function demandDateFor(S, norm, n) { const d = S.demands.find(x => x.flat === norm && x.milestone === n && (x.issued || x.ok !== false)); return d ? d.date : null; }

function computeFlat(S, norm, ctx) {
  ctx = ctx || { adj: bankAdj(S), asOf: asOfDate(S), c: cfg(S) };
  const { adj, asOf, c } = ctx;
  const u = S.unitMap.get(norm) || null, s = S.sales.get(norm) || null, cm = S.customers.get(norm) || {};
  const a = adj.get(norm) || { inst: 0, sd: 0, gst: 0, rounding:0, bounced: 0, items: [] };
  const R = { norm, unit: u, sale: s, cust: cm, area: u ? u.area : 0, sold: !!s };
  if (!s) { R.status = u && u.saleable ? 'unsold' : 'refuge'; R.flags = []; return R; }
  R.av = s.av; R.recv = s.recv + a.inst; R.out = s.av - R.recv; R.pctOut = s.av > 0 ? R.out / s.av * 100 : 0; R.pctRecv = s.av > 0 ? R.recv / s.av * 100 : 0;
  R.rate = s.av > 0 && R.area > 0 ? s.av / R.area : 0;
  R.gst = s.gst + S.demands.filter(d=>d.flat===norm&&(d.issued||d.ok)).reduce((a,d)=>a+(d.newGst||0),0); R.gstRecd = s.gstRecd + a.gst; R.gstBal = R.gst - R.gstRecd;
  R.sd = s.sd; R.sdRecd = s.sdRecd + a.sd; R.sdBal = s.sd - R.sdRecd;
  R.bounced = s.bounced + a.bounced; R.bankBounced = a.bounced; R.bankItems = a.items;
  // schedule allocation
  const pcts = scheduleFor(S, norm);
  let cumPrev = 0, interest = 0, overdueSum = 0, pendingDemand = 0, dueSum = 0;
  R.rows = S.schedule.map((m, i) => {
    const amt = s.av * pcts[i];
    const cumDue = cumPrev + amt;
    const paid = Math.max(0, Math.min(amt, R.recv - cumPrev));
    const unpaid = amt - paid;
    const dDate = demandDateFor(S, norm, m.n);
    const raised = !!dDate || m.demandRaised;
    let demandDate = dDate || (m.demandRaised ? m.date : null);
    if (demandDate && cm.agreementDate && cm.agreementDate > demandDate) demandDate = cm.agreementDate;
    const dueDate = m.achieved && demandDate ? addDays(demandDate, c.dueDays) : null;
    const row = { n: m.n, name: m.name, short: shortName(m.name), pct: pcts[i], amt, cumDue, paid, unpaid, achieved: m.achieved, date: m.date, raised, demandDate, dueDate, status: 'Upcoming', interest: 0, days: 0 };
    cumPrev = cumDue; return row;
  });
  const totalUnpaidAch = R.rows.filter(r => r.achieved).reduce((x, r) => x + r.unpaid, 0);
  const material = totalUnpaidAch > c.tol;
  R.rows.forEach(r => {
    if (!r.achieved) { r.status = 'Upcoming'; return; }
    if (!material || r.unpaid < 1) { r.status = r.unpaid < 1 || !material ? 'Paid' : r.status; if (r.status === 'Paid') return; }
    if (!r.raised) { r.status = 'Demand pending'; pendingDemand += r.unpaid; return; }
    if (r.dueDate && r.dueDate < asOf) { r.status = 'Overdue'; r.days = daysBetween(r.dueDate, asOf); r.interest = r.unpaid * c.rate / 100 * r.days / 365; overdueSum += r.unpaid; interest += r.interest; }
    else { r.status = 'Due'; dueSum += r.unpaid; }
  });
  R.overdue = overdueSum; R.interest = interest; R.pendingDemand = pendingDemand; R.dueNotOverdue = dueSum;
  R.achievedPct = (() => { let x = 0; S.schedule.forEach((m, i) => { if (m.achieved) x += pcts[i]; }); return x; })();
  // flags
  const F = []; R.flags = F;
  const tol = c.tol;
  if (R.overdue > tol) F.push({ sev: 'red', code: 'OVERDUE', title: 'Instalment overdue ' + cr(R.overdue), detail: R.rows.filter(r => r.status === 'Overdue').map(r => r.short + ' (due ' + fmtDate(r.dueDate) + ', ' + r.days + ' days)').join('; ') + '. Interest accruing ' + rs(R.interest) + ' at ' + c.rate + '% p.a.', refs: ['RERA-19-6', 'RERA-19-7', 'RULE-18', 'AGR-PAYMENT', 'AGR-DEFAULT'], amount: R.overdue });
  if (R.bounced > 0) { if (totalUnpaidAch > c.tol) F.push({ sev: 'red', code: 'BOUNCE', title: 'Cheque returned, not replaced ' + cr(R.bounced), detail: 'A cheque of ' + rs(R.bounced) + ' was returned unpaid and the achieved-stage dues are still short by ' + rs(totalUnpaidAch) + '. Issue a reminder with interest and recover cheque-return charges as per the agreement.', refs: ['AGR-DISHONOUR', 'RERA-19-6', 'AGR-DEFAULT'], amount: R.bounced });
    else F.push({ sev: 'info', code: 'BOUNCE_R', title: 'Cheque returned and replaced ' + cr(R.bounced), detail: 'A returned cheque of ' + rs(R.bounced) + ' is recorded, but all achieved-stage dues are now paid. No overdue; keep the replacement evidence on file.', refs: ['AGR-DISHONOUR'], amount: R.bounced }); }
  { const thr = num(infoGet(S, 'TDS 194-IA Threshold (Rs)', 5000000)), trate = num(infoGet(S, 'TDS 194-IA Rate', 0.01));
    R.tdsExpected = s.av >= thr ? Math.round(s.recv * trate) : 0; R.tdsRecorded = (s.cr && s.cr.tds) || 0; R.tdsGap = Math.max(0, R.tdsExpected - R.tdsRecorded);
  }
  if (s.av <= 0 && R.recv > 0) F.push({ sev: 'red', code: 'NOAV', title: 'Receipts ' + cr(R.recv) + ' with no sale value booked', detail: 'Money is received but the Sales JV / agreement value is not recorded. If the amount exceeds 10% of the eventual price, a registered agreement is required first.', refs: ['RERA-13-1', 'AGR-CONSIDERATION'], amount: R.recv });
  else if (s.av > 0 && !cm.registered && R.recv > 0.10 * s.av + tol) F.push({ sev: 'red', code: 'S13', title: 'Collected ' + pct1(R.pctRecv) + ' without a registered agreement', detail: 'Agreement is marked as not registered, yet ' + rs(R.recv) + ' (' + pct1(R.pctRecv) + ' of the agreement value) has been received. The limit before registration is 10%.', refs: ['RERA-13-1', 'AGR-CONSIDERATION'], amount: R.recv - 0.10 * s.av });
  if (R.pendingDemand > tol) F.push({ sev: 'info', code: 'DEMAND', title: 'Demand to be raised ' + cr(R.pendingDemand), detail: R.rows.filter(r => r.status === 'Demand pending').map(r => r.short + ' achieved on ' + fmtDate(r.date)).join('; ') + '. Raise the demand letter from the Demand Letters tab.', refs: ['AGR-PAYMENT', 'RERA-19-6'], amount: R.pendingDemand });
  if (R.gstBal > tol) F.push({ sev: 'amber', code: 'GST', title: 'GST balance ' + cr(R.gstBal), detail: 'GST billed ' + rs(R.gst) + ', received ' + rs(R.gstRecd) + '.', refs: ['AGR-TAXES'], amount: R.gstBal });
  if (R.sdBal > tol) F.push({ sev: 'amber', code: 'SD', title: 'Stamp duty / registration due ' + cr(R.sdBal), detail: 'Billed ' + rs(R.sd) + ', received ' + rs(R.sdRecd) + '.', refs: ['RERA-19-6', 'AGR-STAMP'], amount: R.sdBal });
  R.red = F.filter(f => f.sev === 'red').length; R.amber = F.filter(f => f.sev === 'amber').length; R.info = F.filter(f => f.sev === 'info').length;
  R.status = s.av <= 0 ? 'booked' : R.red ? 'red' : R.amber ? 'amber' : 'ok';
  return R;
}
function computeAll(S) {
  const ctx = { adj: bankAdj(S), asOf: asOfDate(S), c: cfg(S) };
  const flats = new Map();
  S.units.forEach(u => flats.set(u.norm, computeFlat(S, u.norm, ctx)));
  S.sales.forEach((s, k) => { if (!flats.has(k)) flats.set(k, computeFlat(S, k, ctx)); });
  const list = [...flats.values()];
  const sold = list.filter(f => f.sold), saleable = list.filter(f => f.unit && f.unit.saleable);
  const T = { ctx, flats, list, sold, units: S.units.length, saleable: saleable.length, soldN: sold.length, unsoldN: saleable.length - sold.length,
    areaTotal: saleable.reduce((x, f) => x + f.area, 0), areaSold: sold.reduce((x, f) => x + f.area, 0) };
  T.areaUnsold = T.areaTotal - T.areaSold;
  T.av = sold.reduce((x, f) => x + f.av, 0); T.recv = sold.reduce((x, f) => x + f.recv, 0);
  const withAv = sold.filter(f => f.av > 0);
  T.matchedRecv = withAv.reduce((x, f) => x + f.recv, 0); T.unallocatedRecv = T.recv - T.matchedRecv;
  T.out = withAv.reduce((x, f) => x + f.out, 0); T.pctOut = T.av > 0 ? T.out / T.av * 100 : 0;
  const rateFlats = withAv.filter(f => f.area > 0);
  const rateArea = rateFlats.reduce((x, f) => x + f.area, 0);
  T.rateAvg = rateArea > 0 ? rateFlats.reduce((x, f) => x + f.av, 0) / rateArea : 0;
  T.estimateFlats = saleable.filter(f => !f.sold || !(f.av > 0)).map(f => ({ norm:f.norm, area:f.area, category:f.sold?'Booked · AV missing':'Unsold', amount:f.area*T.rateAvg }));
  T.estimatedArea = T.estimateFlats.reduce((x,f)=>x+f.area,0);
  T.estimatedSale = T.estimateFlats.reduce((x,f)=>x+f.amount,0);
  T.projectedRevenue = T.av + T.estimatedSale;
  T.projectionAvailable = rateArea > 0;
  T.projectionMissingArea = sold.filter(f => !(f.av > 0) && !(f.area > 0)).length;

  T.overdue = sold.reduce((x, f) => x + (f.overdue || 0), 0); T.interest = sold.reduce((x, f) => x + (f.interest || 0), 0);
  T.pendingDemand = sold.reduce((x, f) => x + (f.pendingDemand || 0), 0);
  T.redFlats = sold.filter(f => f.red > 0).length; T.amberFlats = sold.filter(f => !f.red && f.amber > 0).length;
  T.flagCount = {}; sold.forEach(f => f.flags.forEach(g => { (T.flagCount[g.code] = T.flagCount[g.code] || { n: 0, amt: 0, sev: g.sev, flats: [], refs: g.refs }); T.flagCount[g.code].n++; T.flagCount[g.code].amt += g.amount || 0; T.flagCount[g.code].flats.push(f.norm); }));
  // cost
  const bankCost = {}; S.txns.filter(t => t.kind === 'cost').forEach(t => { bankCost[t.cat] = (bankCost[t.cat] || 0) + t.dr; });
  T.costRows = S.cost.map(r => ({ head: r.head, budget: r.budget, base: r.incurred, bank: bankCost[r.head] || 0, incurred: r.incurred }));
  T.costBudget = T.costRows.reduce((x, r) => x + r.budget, 0); T.costIncurred = T.costRows.reduce((x, r) => x + r.incurred, 0);
  // 70% test
  const inst = S.txns.filter(t => t.kind === 'instalment' || t.kind === 'demand_payment' || t.kind === 'unmatched').reduce((x, t) => x + (t.kind==='demand_payment'?splitDemandReceipt(S,t).principal:t.cr), 0);
  const ret = S.txns.filter(t => t.kind === 'chq_return').reduce((x, t) => x + t.dr, 0);
  const toRera = S.txns.filter(t => t.kind === 'transfer_rera').reduce((x, t) => x + t.dr, 0);
  const inRera = S.txns.filter(t => t.kind === 'transfer_in' && /RERA|COLLECTION/i.test(t.narr)).reduce((x, t) => x + t.cr, 0);
  T.rera = { base: inst - ret, required: 0.7 * (inst - ret), moved: toRera || inRera, has: S.txns.length > 0 && (inst - ret) > 0 };
  T.rera.gap = T.rera.required - T.rera.moved;
  // billed vs collected per milestone (sold flats with AV)
  T.msBill = S.schedule.map((m, i) => {
    let due = 0, got = 0;
    withAv.forEach(f => { const r = f.rows[i]; due += r.cumDue; got += Math.min(f.recv, r.cumDue); });
    return { n: m.n, short: shortName(m.name), name: m.name, pct: m.pct, achieved: m.achieved, date: m.date, cumDue: due, cumGot: got, gap: due - got, covPct: due > 0 ? got / due * 100 : 100, cumPctOfAv: T.av > 0 ? due / withAv.reduce((x, f) => x + f.av, 0) * 100 : 0 };
  });
  T.achievedPct = S.schedule.reduce((x, m) => x + (m.achieved ? m.pct : 0), 0);
  T.stageName = (() => { let last = null; S.schedule.forEach(m => { if (m.achieved) last = m; }); return last ? last.name : 'Not started'; })();
  upgradeT(S, T);
  return T;
}

/* ---------------- Demand computation & letter ---------------- */
const isInterState=cm=>!!(cm&&cm.state&&!/maharashtra/i.test(String(cm.state)));
function gstOn(principalPaise,inter){ if(inter){const igst=Math.round(principalPaise*5/100);return {igst,cgst:0,sgst:0,total:igst/100};} const c=Math.round(principalPaise*2.5/100);return {igst:0,cgst:c,sgst:c,total:2*c/100}; }
function demandFor(S, T, norm, milestone) {
  const f = T.flats.get(norm); if (!f || !f.sold || f.av <= 0) return null;
  const c = T.ctx.c; const m = f.rows[milestone - 1]; if (!m || !m.achieved) return null;
  if(infoGet(S,'Demo Scenario','')==='MILES2COLLECT-33-5') {
    const round=n=>Math.round((n+Number.EPSILON)*100)/100;
    const principalPaise=Math.round(f.av*m.pct*100),principal=principalPaise/100,demandTax=Math.round(principalPaise*(c.gstRate*100)/100)/100;
    const gst=Math.abs(c.gstRate-.05)<1e-8?gstOn(principalPaise,isInterState(S.customers.get(norm))).total:demandTax;
    const rounding=round(demandTax-gst);
    if(principal<=c.tol)return null;
    return {flat:norm,label:(S.sales.get(norm)||{}).label||norm,customer:(S.sales.get(norm)||{}).customer,email:(S.customers.get(norm)||{}).email||'',milestone,mName:m.name,mShort:m.short,mDate:m.date,pct:m.pct,inst:principal,arrears:0,interest:0,gst,newGst:gst,gstArrears:0,sd:0,rounding,total:round(principal+gst+rounding),principal};
  }
  const inst = m.unpaid;
  const arrears = f.rows.filter(r => r.n < milestone && (r.status === 'Overdue' || r.status === 'Due' || r.status === 'Demand pending')).reduce((x, r) => x + r.unpaid, 0);
  const interest = f.rows.filter(r => r.n < milestone).reduce((x, r) => x + (r.interest || 0), 0);
  const existing=S.demands.find(d=>d.flat===norm && d.milestone===milestone);
  const newGst=Math.round(inst*c.gstRate*100)/100;
  const gstArrears=Math.max(0,f.gstBal-(existing?existing.newGst||0:0));
  const gst=newGst+gstArrears, sd = f.sdBal > c.tol ? f.sdBal : 0;
  const principal = inst + arrears;
  if (principal <= c.tol) return null;
  return { flat: norm, label: (S.unitMap.get(norm) || {}).no || (S.sales.get(norm) || {}).label || norm, customer: (S.sales.get(norm) || {}).customer, email: (S.customers.get(norm) || {}).email || '', milestone, mName: m.name, mShort: m.short, mDate: m.date, pct: m.pct, inst, arrears, interest, gst, newGst, gstArrears, sd, total: principal + interest + gst + sd, principal };
}
function collectionBank(S){
 const promoter=infoGet(S,'Builder / Promoter','Developer');
 let name=String(infoGet(S,'Collection Account Name',promoter+' RERA Collection Account'));
 if(!/RERA Collection Account$/i.test(name))name+=' RERA Collection Account';
 return {name,bank:infoGet(S,'Collection Bank Name','Not provided'),account:infoGet(S,'Collection Account Number','Not provided'),ifsc:infoGet(S,'Collection IFSC','Not provided'),branch:infoGet(S,'Collection Bank Branch','Not provided')};
}
function letterModel(S, T, d, seq, today) {
  const f = T.flats.get(d.flat), c = T.ctx.c, cm = S.customers.get(d.flat) || {};
  const invDate = (d.mDate && d.mDate <= today) ? d.mDate : today;
  const due = addDays(today, c.dueDays);
  const co = { name: infoGet(S, 'Builder / Promoter', 'Developer'), l1: infoGet(S, 'Registered Office Line 1', ''), l2: infoGet(S, 'Registered Office Line 2', ''), cin: infoGet(S, 'CIN (sample)', infoGet(S, 'CIN', '')), gstin: infoGet(S, 'GSTIN (sample)', infoGet(S, 'GSTIN', '')), rera: infoGet(S, 'MahaRERA Registration No (sample)', infoGet(S, 'MahaRERA Registration No', '')), signatory: infoGet(S, 'Authorised Signatory', 'Authorised Signatory'), desig: infoGet(S, 'Signatory Designation', 'Director'), logo: S.logo || null };
  const proj = infoGet(S, 'Project Name', 'Project');
  const ref = 'M2C-' + d.flat + '-M' + d.milestone;
  const lines = [];
  lines.push(['Instalment: ' + d.mName + ' (' + Math.round(d.pct * 100) + '% of agreement value)', d.inst]);
  if (d.arrears > 0) lines.push(['Arrears of earlier instalments', d.arrears]);
  if (d.interest > 0) lines.push(['Interest on delayed payment @ ' + c.rate + '% p.a. (simple interest)', d.interest]);
  if(d.newGst>0)lines.push(['GST @ '+(c.gstRate*100).toFixed(0)+'% on current milestone instalment',d.newGst]);
  if(d.rounding)lines.push(['Rounding adjustment',d.rounding]);
  if(d.gstArrears>0)lines.push(['GST balance on earlier demands',d.gstArrears]);
  if (d.sd > 0) lines.push(['Stamp duty / registration balance', d.sd]);
  const priorDemanded=f.rows.filter(r=>r.n<d.milestone&&r.achieved&&r.raised).reduce((a,r)=>a+r.amt,0);
  return { van: cm.van || '', issued: today, invoice:demoInvoiceModel(S,T,d,invDate), accountAsOf:T.ctx.asOf, priorDemanded, priorBalance:Math.max(0,priorDemanded-f.recv), bank:collectionBank(S), ref, date: invDate, due, co, proj, location: infoGet(S, 'Project Location', ''), flat: d.label, customer: d.customer, address: cm.address || '', email: d.email, agDate: cm.agreementDate, agReg: cm.regNo, area: f.area, av: f.av, recv: f.recv, d, lines, total: d.total, rate: c.rate, dueDays: c.dueDays, subject: 'Demo Tax Invoice - ' + d.mName };
}
function customerLetterRows(L){return [
 ['Customer',L.customer],['Flat / Project',L.flat+' / '+L.proj],['Carpet area',inr(L.area)+' sq ft'],
 ['Agreement date / Registration',(L.agDate?fmtDate(L.agDate):'Not recorded')+' / '+(L.agReg||'Not recorded')],
 ['Agreement value',rs(L.av)],['Principal demanded before this stage',rs(L.priorDemanded)],
 ['Principal received as on '+fmtDate(L.accountAsOf||L.date),rs(L.recv)],['Unpaid principal on earlier demands',rs(L.priorBalance)]
 ];}
function letterCustomerTable(L){return '<h3 style="font-size:14px;margin:12px 0 5px">Customer account summary</h3><table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:14px">'+customerLetterRows(L).map(([k,v])=>'<tr><th style="text-align:left;width:43%;padding:5px 9px;border:1px solid #c9ced6;background:#f0f3f7">'+esc(k)+'</th><td style="padding:5px 9px;border:1px solid #c9ced6">'+esc(v)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</table><div style="font-size:11px">Account summary is principal only; GST and stamp duty are separate. Current-stage demand is detailed below.</div>';}
function demoInvoiceModel(S,T,d,date){
 const fyStart=date.getUTCFullYear()-(date.getUTCMonth()<3?1:0),fy=String(fyStart).slice(-2)+String(fyStart+1).slice(-2);
 if(!S.invoiceDrafts)S.invoiceDrafts={};if(!S.invoiceCounters)S.invoiceCounters={};
 const key=fy+'-'+d.flat+'-'+d.milestone;
 if(!S.invoiceDrafts[key]){const next=(S.invoiceCounters[fy]||0)+1;if(next>99999)throw new Error('Demo invoice series exhausted');S.invoiceCounters[fy]=next;S.invoiceDrafts[key]='DEMO/'+fy+'/'+String(next).padStart(5,'0');}
 const money=n=>Math.round((Number(n)+Number.EPSILON)*100)/100;
 const gross=money(d.inst),grossPaise=Math.round(gross*100),cmx=S.customers.get(d.flat)||{},inter=isInterState(cmx),sp=gstOn(grossPaise,inter),taxable=gross,cgst=sp.cgst/100,sgst=sp.sgst/100,igst=sp.igst/100;
 const currentTax=money(d.newGst||0),rounding=money(currentTax+(d.rounding||0)-cgst-sgst-igst);
 const earlier=money(d.total-gross-currentTax-(d.rounding||0));
 const cm=S.customers.get(d.flat)||{};
 return {number:S.invoiceDrafts[key],fy,description:'Residential apartment construction service — '+d.mName,sac:infoGet(S,'Service SAC','995411'),supplierState:'Maharashtra',supplierCode:'27',place:inter?String(cm.state):'Maharashtra',placeCode:inter?'—':'27',recipientGstin:cm.gstin||'Not provided — confirm registration status',gross,taxable,cgst,sgst,igst,inter,rate:inter?5:2.5,rounding,earlier,total:money(d.total),reverseCharge:'No (demo assumption)',basis:'Non-affordable residential; GST 5% without ITC on the instalment (effective rate; the land-value deduction is already built into the 5%, so no separate one-third deduction): CGST 2.5% + SGST 2.5% when intra-State, IGST 5% when inter-State.',ready:false};
}
function invoiceAmountRows(L){const v=L.invoice;return [
 ['Current milestone instalment',v.gross],
 ['Discount',0],['Taxable value (no land deduction)',v.taxable],
 ...(v.inter?[['IGST @ 5%',v.igst]]:[['CGST @ 2.5%',v.cgst],['SGST @ 2.5%',v.sgst]]),
 ...(v.rounding?[['Tax rounding adjustment',v.rounding]]:[]),
 ...(v.earlier?[['Earlier dues / other balances — not newly taxed here',v.earlier]]:[]),
 ['Total amount payable',v.total]
 ];}
function invoiceHeaderRows(L){const v=L.invoice;return [['Demo invoice number',v.number],['Invoice date (= milestone completion, CGST s.31(5)(c))',fmtDate(L.date)],['Issued / communicated on',fmtDate(L.issued||L.date)],['Demand / payment reference',L.ref],['Place of supply / State code',v.place+' / '+v.placeCode],['Supplier State / code',v.supplierState+' / '+v.supplierCode],['SAC',v.sac],['Recipient GSTIN / UIN',v.recipientGstin],['Reverse charge',v.reverseCharge]];}
function invoiceHtmlTable(rows){return '<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px;margin:8px 0">'+rows.map(([k,v])=>'<tr><th style="width:44%;text-align:left;background:#f0f3f7;border:1px solid #c8d1db;padding:5px 8px">'+esc(k)+'</th><td style="border:1px solid #c8d1db;padding:5px 8px">'+esc(v)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</table>';}
const inr2=n=>Number(n||0).toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
function certInfoFor(L){const x=S.flow||{},c=x.cert;if(c&&c.fields&&c.fields.certificateRef&&+x.milestone===+L.d.milestone&&!L.d.catchup)return {ref:c.fields.certificateRef,architect:c.fields.architect||''};return null;}
/* One data model for the tax invoice-cum-demand, used by the on-screen preview and by the PDF */
function invoiceDemandData(L){
 const v=L.invoice,ci=certInfoFor(L),N=S.schedule.length,r2=n=>Math.round((Number(n)+Number.EPSILON)*100)/100;
 const taxHead=v.inter?['IGST '+v.rate+'%']:['CGST 2.5%','SGST 2.5%'];
 const items=[{desc:'Construction-linked instalment: Stage '+L.d.milestone+' of '+N+', '+L.d.mName+' ('+pct1(L.d.pct*100)+' of agreement value)',sac:v.sac,taxable:v.taxable,tax:v.inter?[v.igst]:[v.cgst,v.sgst],total:r2(v.taxable+(v.inter?v.igst:v.cgst+v.sgst))}];
 if(v.rounding)items.push({desc:'Tax rounding adjustment',sac:'',taxable:null,tax:null,total:v.rounding});
 if(v.earlier)items.push({desc:'Earlier dues and other balances (arrears, interest, GST on earlier demands, stamp duty / registration). Not newly taxed here.',sac:'',taxable:null,tax:null,total:v.earlier});
 const invoice=[['Invoice no.',v.number],['Invoice date',fmtDate(L.date)+' (stage completion)'],['Demand ref.',L.ref],['Issued on',fmtDate(L.issued||L.date)]];
 const supply=[['Service / SAC','Construction of residential apartment / '+v.sac],['Place of supply',v.place+(v.placeCode&&v.placeCode!=='—'?' ('+v.placeCode+')':'')],['Reverse charge',String(v.reverseCharge).replace(/\s*\(.*\)/,'')],['Due date',fmtDate(L.due)]];
 const bill=[L.customer,L.address||'Address not provided','Property: Flat '+L.flat+', '+L.proj+(L.location?', '+L.location:''),'Recipient GSTIN: '+v.recipientGstin];
 const agr=['Agreement for Sale dated '+(L.agDate?fmtDate(L.agDate):'not recorded')+' | Reg. No. '+(L.agReg||'not recorded'),'Agreement value '+rs(L.av)+(L.area?' | Carpet area '+inr(L.area)+' sq ft':''),'Stage '+L.d.milestone+' of '+N+(L.d.mDate?' certified '+fmtDate(L.d.mDate):'')+(ci?' | Architect certificate '+ci.ref:'')];
 const strip=[['Agreement value',rs(L.av)],['Demanded before this stage',rs(L.priorDemanded)],['Principal received to '+fmtDate(L.accountAsOf||L.date),rs(L.recv)],['Unpaid on earlier demands',rs(L.priorBalance)]];
 const bank=[['Account name',L.bank.name],['Bank / Branch',L.bank.bank+' / '+L.bank.branch],['Account no.',L.bank.account],['IFSC',L.bank.ifsc],...(L.van?[['Virtual A/c (auto-credit)',L.van]]:[]),['Narration',L.ref]];
 const terms=['Payable within '+L.dueDays+' days, by '+fmtDate(L.due)+'. Quote '+L.ref+' in the payment narration.','Interest at '+L.rate+'% p.a. (simple) on delayed payment, from the due date until payment.','GST at 5% without input tax credit ('+(v.inter?'IGST 5%':'CGST 2.5% + SGST 2.5%')+'). The land-value deduction is already built into the 5%.','TDS, if applicable, to be deposited by the allottee; please share the challan / Form 16B.','Payment by NEFT / RTGS / cheque / DD only. Timely payment is an obligation of the allottee under Section 19(6) of the RERA Act, 2016.'];
 const ids='CIN '+(L.co.cin||'not provided')+' | GSTIN '+(L.co.gstin||'NOT PROVIDED')+' | MahaRERA '+(L.co.rera||'not provided');
 return {v,items,taxHead,invoice,supply,bill,agr,strip,bank,terms,ids,words:'Rupees '+amountInWords(v.total)+' only',total:v.total,sign:[L.co.signatory+', '+L.co.desig,'Authorised signatory (demonstration, not digitally signed)'],note:'Demonstration with fictional data. Supplier GSTIN, recipient tax status and e-invoice / IRN requirements must be completed and reviewed before live use. No invoice is registered with GST systems by this tool.'};
}
function letterHtml(L){
 const D=invoiceDemandData(L),B='1.5px solid #17222d',b1='1px solid #17222d';
 const kv=rows=>'<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11.5px">'+rows.map(([k,x])=>'<tr><td style="color:#5d6b7a;width:118px;vertical-align:top;padding:1px 6px 1px 0">'+esc(k)+'</td><td style="padding:1px 0">'+esc(x)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</table>';
 const h5=t=>'<div style="font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.07em;color:#5d6b7a;margin-bottom:3px">'+t+'</div>';
 const nt=D.taxHead.length;
 const tcell=(x,al)=>'<td style="border:'+b1+';padding:7px;text-align:'+(al||'right')+';white-space:nowrap">'+x+'</td>';
 const rows=D.items.map(it=>'<tr><td style="border:'+b1+';padding:7px">'+esc(it.desc)+'</td>'+tcell(esc(it.sac),'left')+(it.taxable==null?'<td colspan="'+(1+nt)+'" style="border:'+b1+'"></td>':tcell(inr2(it.taxable))+it.tax.map(t=>tcell(inr2(t))).join(''))+tcell(inr2(it.total))+'</tr>').join('');
 const totTax=D.items.filter(i=>i.tax);
 const sumTax=D.taxHead.map((_,k)=>totTax.reduce((a,i)=>a+i.tax[k],0));
 const sumTaxable=D.items.reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.taxable||0),0);
 const tot='<tr style="font-weight:700;background:#e8edf2"><td colspan="2" style="border:'+b1+';padding:7px">Total amount payable</td>'+tcell(inr2(sumTaxable))+sumTax.map(t=>tcell(inr2(t))).join('')+tcell(inr2(D.total))+'</tr>';
 const th=(t,al)=>'<th style="border:'+b1+';padding:6px 7px;background:#e8edf2;font-size:11px;text-align:'+(al||'right')+'">'+t+'</th>';
 const cell=(inner,r,bt)=>'<td style="width:50%;vertical-align:top;padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:'+b1+(r?';border-right:'+b1:'')+'">'+inner+'</td>';
 return '<article style="font-family:Arial,sans-serif;color:#17222d;max-width:780px;margin:auto;font-size:12px;line-height:1.4;border:'+B+'">'
 +'<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;padding:10px 12px;border-bottom:'+B+'"><div><div style="font-size:17px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase">'+esc(L.co.name)+'</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:#3b4856">'+esc((L.co.l1+' '+L.co.l2).trim())+'<br>'+esc(D.ids)+'</div></div><div style="text-align:right"><div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em">Tax Invoice-cum-Demand</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:#3b4856">Original for recipient</div><div style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;color:#a52828;margin-top:3px">DEMONSTRATION: FICTIONAL DATA</div></div></div>'
 +'<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse"><tr>'+cell(h5('Invoice details')+kv(D.invoice),true)+cell(h5('Supply details')+kv(D.supply))+'</tr><tr>'+cell(h5('Bill to (allottee)')+'<b>'+esc(D.bill[0])+'</b><br>'+D.bill.slice(1).map(esc).join('<br>'),true)+cell(h5('Agreement')+D.agr.map(esc).join('<br>'))+'</tr></table>'
 +'<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px"><thead><tr>'+th('Description of service','left')+th('SAC','left')+th('Taxable value (₹)')+D.taxHead.map(h=>th(h+' (₹)')).join('')+th('Total (₹)')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows+tot+'</tbody></table>'
 +'<div style="padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:'+b1+';font-weight:600">Amount in words: '+esc(D.words)+'</div>'
 +'<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11.5px"><tr>'+D.strip.map(([k,x],i)=>'<td style="padding:6px 12px;border-bottom:'+b1+(i<3?';border-right:'+b1:'')+'"><div style="font-size:9.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;color:#5d6b7a">'+esc(k)+'</div><b>'+esc(x)+'</b></td>').join('')+'</tr></table>'
 +'<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse"><tr><td style="width:50%;vertical-align:top;padding:9px 12px;border-right:'+b1+'">'+h5('Pay to')+kv(D.bank)+'</td><td style="vertical-align:top;padding:9px 12px">'+h5('Terms')+'<ol style="margin:3px 0 0 16px;padding:0;font-size:11px">'+D.terms.map(t=>'<li style="margin-bottom:2px">'+esc(t)+'</li>').join('')+'</ol></td></tr></table>'
 +'<div style="text-align:right;padding:9px 12px;border-top:'+b1+';min-height:78px">For <b>'+esc(L.co.name)+'</b><div style="margin-top:32px;font-weight:700">'+esc(D.sign[0])+'</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#5d6b7a">'+esc(D.sign[1])+'</div></div></article>'
 +'<p style="max-width:780px;margin:8px auto 0;font-size:10px;color:#5d6b7a;text-align:center;font-family:Arial,sans-serif">'+esc(D.note)+'</p>';
}

/* ================= END LOGIC ================= */

/* ---------------- PDF text extraction (works with pdf.js in browser and node) ---------------- */
async function pdfToLines(pdfjsLib, data, password) {
  const task = pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data, password: password || undefined, useSystemFonts: true, isEvalSupported: false });
  const doc = await task.promise;
  const lines = [];
  for (let p = 1; p <= doc.numPages; p++) {
    const page = await doc.getPage(p);
    const tc = await page.getTextContent();
    const items = tc.items.filter(i => i.str && i.str.trim() !== '').map(i => ({ x: i.transform[4], y: i.transform[5], w: i.width || 0, s: i.str.trim() }));
    items.sort((a, b) => b.y - a.y || a.x - b.x);
    let cur = null;
    items.forEach(it => {
      if (cur && Math.abs(cur.y - it.y) <= 2.5) { cur.items.push(it); }
      else { cur = { y: it.y, items: [it] }; lines.push(cur); }
    });
  }
  return lines.map(l => l.items.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x).map(i => ({ x: i.x, w: i.w, s: i.s })));
}

/* ---------- drawer ---------- */
function openRef(code, flat) {
  const r = REFS[code]; if (!r) return;
  const f = flat ? T.flats.get(flat) : null;
  const flag = f && f.flags.find(g => g.refs.includes(code));
  let h = '';
  if (r.kind === 'act') {
    h += '<h5>What the law says</h5><p>' + esc(r.body) + '</p>';
    if (flag) h += '<h5>Why it is flagged for Flat ' + esc(flatLabel(flat)) + '</h5><div class="why"><b>' + esc(flag.title) + '</b><br>' + esc(flag.detail) + '</div>';
    else if (!flat) { const codes = Object.entries(T.flagCount).filter(([k, v]) => v.refs.includes(code)); if (codes.length) h += '<h5>Flagged in this project</h5><div class="why">' + codes.map(([k, v]) => '<b>' + esc(FLAGTITLE[k] || k) + '</b>: ' + v.n + ' flat(s), ' + cr(v.amt)).join('<br>') + '</div>'; }
    h += '<h5>Official source</h5><div class="lnk">' + r.links.map(l => '<a href="' + l[1] + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + esc(l[0]) + ' &#8599;</a>').join('') + '</div>';
    h += '<p class="hint" style="margin-top:14px">Summary in plain words for quick reference. The Act and Rules as published are the authority; check the current text before acting.</p>';
  } else {
    const cm = f ? (S.customers.get(flat) || {}) : {};
    h += '<h5>Clause in the Agreement for Sale</h5><p><b>' + esc(r.clause) + '</b>' + (f ? '<br><span class="hint">Flat ' + esc(flatLabel(flat)) + ' · ' + esc((f.sale || {}).customer) + (cm.agreementDate ? ' · Agreement dated ' + fmtDate(cm.agreementDate) : '') + (cm.regNo ? ' · Reg. No. ' + esc(cm.regNo) : '') + '</span>' : '') + '</p>';
    h += '<div class="quote">' + esc(r.text) + '</div>';
    if (flag) h += '<h5>Why it is flagged</h5><div class="why"><b>' + esc(flag.title) + '</b><br>' + esc(flag.detail) + '</div>';
    h += '<p class="hint" style="margin-top:12px">Illustrative clause wording for the demonstration. In live use this opens the clause from the customer\'s registered Agreement for Sale; the executed agreement governs.</p>';
    if (f) h += '<div class="lnk"><a href="#" data-act="goCust" data-flat="' + flat + '">Open Customer Payment Schedule &#8594;</a></div>';
  }
  $('#dTitle').textContent = r.title; $('#dSub').textContent = r.sub; $('#dBody').innerHTML = h;
  $('#drawer').classList.add('on'); $('#veil').classList.add('on'); $('#drawer').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
}
function closeDrawer() { $('#drawer').classList.remove('on'); $('#veil').classList.remove('on'); $('#drawer').setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); }

/* ---------- small chart helpers ---------- */
function donut(p, col, label) {
  const c = 2 * Math.PI * 52; const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, p)) / 100 * c;
  return '<div class="ring" style="width:130px;height:130px"><svg width="130" height="130" viewBox="0 0 130 130"><circle cx="65" cy="65" r="52" fill="none" stroke="#e8ecf1" stroke-width="13"/><circle cx="65" cy="65" r="52" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="13" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="' + v.toFixed(1) + ' ' + c.toFixed(1) + '"/></svg><b style="font-size:22px">' + label + '</b></div>';
}
function ring(p, col) {
  const c = 2 * Math.PI * 34; const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, p)) / 100 * c;
  return '<div class="ring"><svg width="84" height="84" viewBox="0 0 84 84"><circle cx="42" cy="42" r="34" fill="none" stroke="#e8ecf1" stroke-width="9"/><circle cx="42" cy="42" r="34" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="' + v.toFixed(1) + ' ' + c.toFixed(1) + '"/></svg><b>' + Math.round(p) + '%</b></div>';
}
function segBar(parts) { const tot = parts.reduce((x, p) => x + p.v, 0) || 1; return '<div class="sbar">' + parts.map(p => '<i style="width:' + (p.v / tot * 100) + '%;background:' + p.c + '"></i>').join('') + '</div>'; }

/* ---------- MASTER DASHBOARD ---------- */
function flagTone(code,severity){return code==='NOAV'?'booked':severity;}
function flatStatusLink(norm){
 const f=T.flats.get(norm),status=f?f.status:'unsold';
 const labels={ok:'Sold, no flags',amber:'Sold, GST / stamp duty due',red:'Sold, red flag',booked:'Booked, agreement pending',unsold:'Unsold inventory',refuge:'Refuge area'};
 return '<a class="flat-status-link status-'+status+'" href="#" data-act="openFlat" data-flat="'+norm+'" title="'+esc(labels[status]||status)+' · same colour as inventory map">'+esc(flatLabel(norm))+'</a>';
}
function stackHtml() {
  const maxF = Math.max(0, ...S.units.map(u => u.floor));
  let h = '<div class="roof"></div>';
  for (let fl = maxF; fl >= 1; fl--) {
    const us = S.units.filter(u => u.floor === fl).sort((a, b) => a.norm - b.norm);
    h += '<div class="fl"><div class="n">' + fl + '</div>' + us.map(u => {
      const f = T.flats.get(u.norm); const cls = !f ? 'refuge' : f.status === 'ok' ? 'ok' : f.status;
      const c = { ok: 't-ok', amber: 't-amber', red: 't-red', booked: 't-booked', unsold: 't-unsold', refuge: 't-refuge' }[cls] || 't-ok';
      return '<button class="tile ' + c + '" data-act="tile" data-flat="' + u.norm + '" data-tip="' + u.norm + '"' + (cls === 'refuge' ? ' tabindex="-1"' : '') + '><b>' + esc(u.no) + '</b><span>' + (u.saleable ? inr(u.area) : 'Refuge') + '</span></button>';
    }).join('') + (us.length < 2 ? '<div></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  return h;
}

  const counts={red:0,amber:0,ok:0,booked:0,unsold:0,refuge:0};T.list.forEach(f=>{if(f.status==='refuge')counts.refuge++;else counts[f.status]++;});
  const fc=Object.entries(T.flagCount).sort((a,b)=>({red:0,amber:1,info:2}[a[1].sev])-({red:0,amber:1,info:2}[b[1].sev])||b[1].amt-a[1].amt);
  let h='';
  h += '<div class="card s7"><h3>Inventory map <small>click a flat to open its dashboard</small></h3><div class="stack"><div class="floors">' + stackHtml() + '</div><div class="legend">'
    + '<div><i style="background:var(--teal)"></i>Sold, no flags<b>' + counts.ok + '</b></div><div><i style="background:var(--amber)"></i>Sold, GST / stamp duty due<b>' + counts.amber + '</b></div><div><i style="background:var(--red)"></i>Sold, red flag<b>' + counts.red + '</b></div><div><i style="background:var(--violet)"></i>Booked, agreement pending<b>' + counts.booked + '</b></div><div><i style="border:2px dashed var(--brass);background:#fff"></i>Unsold inventory<b>' + counts.unsold + '</b></div><div><i style="background:repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,#d4d9e0 0 4px,#e5e9ee 4px 8px)"></i>Refuge area<b>' + counts.refuge + '</b></div></div></div></div>';
  h += '<div class="card s5"><h3>Red flags &amp; compliance <small>click a section to read it</small></h3>' + (fc.length ? fc.map(([k, v]) => '<div class="flag ' + flagTone(k,v.sev) + '"><div class="ic">' + (v.sev === 'red' ? '!' : '&#9679;') + '</div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="tt">' + esc(FLAGTITLE[k] || k) + '</div><div style="font-size:12px;color:#3d4a57;margin:3px 0 5px;line-height:1.4">' + esc(FLAGHELP[k] || '') + '</div><div class="dd">' + v.n + ' flat(s) · ' + cr(v.amt) + ' · ' + v.flats.slice(0, 8).map(flatStatusLink).join(' ') + (v.flats.length > 8 ? ' …' : '') + '</div>' + refs(v.refs, '') + '</div></div>').join('') : '<div class="empty">No flags</div>') + '</div>';
  return '<div class="grid g12" style="margin-bottom:14px">'+h+'</div>';
}
