Amounts use the unrounded average rate; displayed rates are rounded.</p>' + (T.projectionMissingArea ? '<div class="note warn">'+T.projectionMissingArea+' booked flat(s) with missing agreement value and area could not be estimated. Projection is incomplete.</div>' : '') + '<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Flat</th><th>Category</th><th class="r">Area sq ft</th><th class="r">Estimated sale ₹</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + T.estimateFlats.map(f=>'<tr><td>'+esc(flatLabel(f.norm))+'</td><td>'+f.category+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(f.area)+'</td><td class="r">'+(T.projectionAvailable?inr(f.amount):'Unavailable')+'</td></tr>').join('') + '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="3">Estimated sale</td><td class="r">'+(T.projectionAvailable?inr(T.estimatedSale):'Unavailable')+'</td></tr><tr><td colspan="3">Recorded agreement value</td><td class="r">'+inr(T.av)+'</td></tr><tr><td colspan="3">Total projected revenue</td><td class="r">'+(T.projectionAvailable?inr(T.projectedRevenue):'Unavailable')+'</td></tr></tfoot></table></div></details></div>';
  h += '<div class="card s12"><h3>Construction journey</h3><div class="ribbon">' + T.msBill.filter(m => !/occupancy|OC received/i.test(m.name+' '+m.short)).map(m => '<div class="step '+(m.achieved?'done':'')+'"><div class="dot">'+(m.achieved?'&#10003;':m.n)+'</div><div class="nm">'+esc(m.short)+'</div><div class="dt">'+(m.date?fmtDate(m.date):'Pending')+'</div></div>').join('') + '</div></div>';
  h += '<div class="card s12"><details><summary style="cursor:pointer;font-weight:700">Interest working · '+rs(T.interest)+' · flat-wise details</summary><p class="hint">Indicative simple interest on current unpaid overdue principal: principal × configured annual rate × delay days / 365. Opening receipts are allocated oldest stage first. This does not reconstruct historical late-payment interest on settled instalments. Rate is an input, not a live rate feed.</p><div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Flat</th><th>Customer</th><th>Milestone</th><th>Due date</th><th>As of</th><th class="r">Principal ₹</th><th class="r">Delay days</th><th class="r">Rate %</th><th class="r">Interest ₹</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + T.sold.filter(f=>f.interest>0).map(f => '<tr style="background:#eef2f6"><td><b>'+esc(flatLabel(f.norm))+'</b></td><td colspan="4">'+esc(f.sale.customer)+' · Flat total</td><td class="r">'+inr(f.overdue)+'</td><td></td><td></td><td class="r"><b>'+inr(f.interest)+'</b></td></tr>'+f.rows.filter(r=>r.interest>0).map(r=>'<tr><td></td><td></td><td>'+esc(r.short)+'</td><td>'+fmtDate(r.dueDate)+'</td><td>'+fmtDate(T.ctx.asOf)+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(r.unpaid)+'</td><td class="r">'+r.days+'</td><td class="r">'+c.rate+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(r.interest)+'</td></tr>').join('')).join('') + '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="8">Total indicative interest (rounded after aggregation)</td><td class="r">'+inr(T.interest)+'</td></tr></tfoot></table></div></details></div>';
  // lower row
  h += '<div class="card s6"><h3>Cost vs budget <small>demo figures</small></h3>' + T.costRows.map(r => '<div style="margin-bottom:9px"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:12.5px"><span>' + esc(r.head) + '</span><b>' + cr(r.incurred) + ' <span class="hint">/ ' + cr(r.budget) + '</span></b></div><div class="hbar" style="position:relative;height:10px;background:#fff;border:1px solid #cfd6df"><i style="width:' + (r.incurred / maxBud * 100) + '%;background:' + (r.incurred > r.budget ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--brass)') + ';position:absolute;left:0;top:0;bottom:0"></i><u style="position:absolute;left:' + (r.budget / maxBud * 100) + '%;top:-2px;bottom:-2px;width:2px;background:var(--navy)"></u></div></div>').join('') + '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:8px"><span class="pill" style="background:#f6ecd2;color:#7a5a12">&#9632; Incurred</span><span class="pill" style="background:#e3e9f1;color:#0f2a44">&#9475; Budget</span><span class="pill" style="background:#f8dcd9;color:#a52828">&#9632; Over budget</span>' + (addedCost ? '<span class="pill" style="background:#eef1f5;color:#4a5866">Bank payments ' + cr(addedCost) + ' · not added to cost</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  h += '<div class="card s6"><h3>Highest outstanding flats</h3>' + topOut.map(f => '<div style="margin-bottom:9px;cursor:pointer" data-act="openFlat" data-flat="' + f.norm + '"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px"><span><b>' + esc(flatLabel(f.norm)) + '</b> · ' + esc(f.sale.customer) + '</span><b>' + cr(f.out) + ' <span class="hint">' + pct1(f.pctOut) + '</span></b></div><div class="hbar"><i style="width:' + (f.out / maxOut * 100) + '%;background:var(--amber)"></i></div></div>').join('') + '</div>';
  h += '</div>';
  return h;
}

/* ---------- FLAT DASHBOARD ---------- */
function flatSelect(id, bind, list, cur) {
  return '<select id="' + id + '" data-bind="' + bind + '" aria-label="Select flat">' + list.map(f => '<option value="' + f.norm + '"' + (f.norm === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>Flat ' + esc(flatLabel(f.norm)) + (f.sale ? ' · ' + esc(f.sale.customer) : ' · unsold') + '</option>').join('') + '</select>';
}
function journeyFlat(f) {
  return '<div class="ribbon">' + f.rows.map((r, i) => {
    const now = r.achieved && !(f.rows[i + 1] && f.rows[i + 1].achieved);
    const pc = r.amt > 0 ? Math.min(100, r.paid / r.amt * 100) : 0;
    return '<div class="step ' + (r.achieved ? 'done' : '') + (now ? ' now' : '') + '" title="' + esc(r.name) + '"><div class="dot">' + (r.achieved ? '&#10003;' : r.n) + '</div><div class="nm">' + esc(r.short) + '</div><div class="pc">' + Math.round(r.pct * 100) + '%</div><div class="dt">' + (r.date ? fmtDate(r.date) : 'Pending') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="rail"><div class="gp">' + cr(r.amt) + '</div><div style="margin-top:4px"><span class="st ' + stCls(r.status) + '">' + r.status + '</span></div>'
      + (r.status === 'Overdue' ? '<div style="margin-top:5px">' + refPill('RERA-19-7', f.norm) + '</div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }).join('') + '</div>';
}
function vFlat() {
  const list = [...T.list.filter(f => f.sold), ...T.list.filter(f => !f.sold && f.unit && f.unit.saleable)];
  if (!T.flats.has(UI.flat)) UI.flat = list[0].norm;
  const f = T.flats.get(UI.flat); const idx = list.findIndex(x => x.norm === UI.flat);
  const proj = infoGet(S, 'Project Name', 'Project');
  let h = '<h2 class="pg">Flat Dashboard</h2><p class="lead">Project details and the construction and payment journey for one flat. Use the selector to move across all flats in the project.</p>';
  h += '<div class="sel"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="prevFlat" aria-label="Previous flat">&#8592;</button>' + flatSelect('selFlat', 'flat', list, UI.flat) + '<button class="btn line sm" data-act="nextFlat" aria-label="Next flat">&#8594;</button><span class="hint">' + (idx + 1) + ' of ' + list.length + '</span></div>';
  if (!f.sold) {
    h += '<div class="card"><h3>Unsold inventory</h3><p><b>Flat ' + esc(f.unit.no) + '</b> · Floor ' + f.unit.floor + ' · ' + inr(f.area) + ' sq ft is unsold stock in ' + esc(proj) + '.</p></div>';
    return h;
  }
  const cm = f.cust; const regYes = cm.registered;
  h += '<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div class="fhead"><div><div class="hint">Flat</div><h2 class="bigtitle">' + esc(flatLabel(f.norm)) + '</h2><div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:15px"><b>' + esc(f.sale.customer) + '</b> · Floor ' + f.unit.floor + ' · ' + inr(f.area) + ' sq ft' + (f.rate ? ' · ' + rs(f.rate) + ' per sq ft' : '') + '</div><div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">' + pill(regYes ? 'ok' : 'red', regYes ? 'Agreement registered' : 'Agreement not registered') + pill('grey', 'Plan ' + esc(cm.plan || 'CLP-STD')) + (f.red ? pill('red', f.red + ' red flag' + (f.red > 1 ? 's' : '')) : '') + (f.amber ? pill('amber', f.amber + ' to review') : '') + (f.info ? pill('grey', 'Demand to raise') : '') + '</div></div>'
    + '<div class="mon"><div><b>' + (f.av > 0 ? cr(f.av) : 'Not booked') + '</b><span>Agreement value</span></div><div><b class="ok">' + cr(f.recv) + '</b><span>Received</span></div><div><b class="' + (f.pctOut > 10 ? 'warn' : '') + '">' + (f.av > 0 ? cr(f.out) : '&mdash;') + '</b><span>Outstanding</span></div>' + (f.av > 0 ? ring(100 - f.pctOut, f.pctOut > 12 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--teal)') : '') + '</div></div></div>';
  h += '<div class="grid g12"><div class="card s6"><h3>Project details</h3><dl class="kv"><dt>Project</dt><dd>' + esc(proj) + '</dd><dt>Location</dt><dd>' + esc(infoGet(S, 'Project Location', '')) + '</dd><dt>MahaRERA No.</dt><dd>' + esc(infoGet(S, 'MahaRERA Registration No (sample)', infoGet(S, 'MahaRERA Registration No', ''))) + '</dd><dt>Promoter</dt><dd>' + esc(infoGet(S, 'Builder / Promoter', '')) + '</dd><dt>RERA completion date</dt><dd>' + esc(infoGet(S, 'Original RERA Completion Date', '')) + '</dd><dt>Construction stage</dt><dd>' + esc(T.stageName) + '</dd><dt>Billing achieved</dt><dd>' + Math.round(f.achievedPct * 100) + '% of agreement value</dd></dl></div>';
  h += '<div class="card s6"><h3>Flat &amp; customer details</h3><dl class="kv"><dt>Customer</dt><dd>' + esc(f.sale.customer) + '</dd><dt>Email</dt><dd>' + esc(cm.email || '—') + '</dd><dt>Mobile</dt><dd>' + esc(cm.mobile || '—') + '</dd><dt>Agreement date</dt><dd>' + (cm.agreementDate ? fmtDate(cm.agreementDate) : '—') + '</dd><dt>Registration no.</dt><dd>' + esc(cm.regNo || '—') + '</dd><dt>Home loan</dt><dd>' + esc(cm.loanBank || '—') + '</dd><dt>GST / Stamp duty</dt><dd>GST bal. ' + rs(f.gstBal) + ' · Stamp duty bal. ' + rs(f.sdBal) + '</dd></dl></div>';
  h += '<div class="card s12"><h3>Construction &amp; Payment journey <small>' + (f.av > 0 ? 'amount per stage = agreement value × stage %' : 'agreement value not booked') + '</small></h3>' + (f.av > 0 ? journeyFlat(f) + '<div class="hint">Upper row: construction milestone. Lower row: amount payable at that stage, how much is paid, and status. Overdue stages link to the RERA provision on interest.</div>' : '<div class="note warn">Sale value is not booked for this flat, so the schedule cannot be computed.</div>') + '</div>';
  h += '<div class="card s7"><h3>Red flags &amp; compliance <small>click a section or clause to open it</small></h3>' + (f.flags.length ? f.flags.map(g => '<div class="flag ' + flagTone(g.code,g.sev) + '"><div class="ic">' + (g.sev === 'red' ? '!' : '&#9679;') + '</div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="tt">' + esc(g.title) + '</div><div class="dd">' + esc(g.detail) + '</div>' + refs(g.refs, f.norm) + '</div></div>').join('') : '<div class="note ok">No flags for this flat.</div>') + '</div>';
  h += '<div class="card s5"><h3>Receipts from bank statements</h3>' + (f.bankItems.length ? '<table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Entry</th><th class="r">Amount</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + f.bankItems.map(t => '<tr><td>' + fmtDate(t.date) + '</td><td>' + esc(t.kind === 'chq_return' ? 'Cheque returned' : t.kind === 'stamp' ? 'Stamp duty' : t.kind === 'gst' ? 'GST' : 'Instalment') + '</td><td class="r ' + (t.kind === 'chq_return' ? 'bad' : '') + '">' + (t.kind === 'chq_return' ? '-' + inr(t.dr) : inr(t.cr)) + '</td></tr>').join('') + '</tbody></table>' : '<div class="empty">No bank statement entries for this flat yet.<br><a href="#" data-act="goTab" data-tab="bank">Upload a statement</a></div>') + '</div></div>';
  return h;
}

/* ---------- SALES & INVENTORY ---------- */
function inventoryCategoryPanels(){
 const av=T.sold.filter(f=>f.av<=0),un=T.list.filter(f=>!f.sold&&f.unit&&f.unit.saleable);
 const sec=(label,list,color,body)=>'<section class="card s6" style="border-top:4px solid '+color+'"><h3>'+label+'<small>'+list.length+' flats · '+inr(list.reduce((a,f)=>a+f.area,0))+' sq ft</small></h3><div class="tw"><table class="t">'+body+'</table></div></section>';
 return '<div class="grid g12 inventory-categories" style="margin-bottom:14px">'
  +sec('Booked — agreement pending',av,'var(--violet)','<thead><tr><th>Flat</th><th>Customer</th><th class="r">Area sq ft</th><th class="r">Advance received ₹</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+av.map(f=>'<tr><td>'+flatStatusLink(f.norm)+'</td><td>'+esc(f.sale.customer)+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(f.area)+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(f.recv)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody>')
  +sec('Unsold inventory',un,'var(--brass)','<thead><tr><th>Flat</th><th>Floor</th><th class="r">Area sq ft</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+un.map(f=>'<tr><td>'+flatStatusLink(f.norm)+'</td><td>'+(f.unit?f.unit.floor:'')+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(f.area)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody>')+'</div>';
}

function vSales() {
  const rows = T.list.filter(f => f.unit ? f.unit.saleable || f.sold : f.sold).map(f => ({ f, norm: f.norm, flat: flatLabel(f.norm), cust: f.sale ? f.sale.customer : 'Unsold', area: f.area, rate: f.rate || 0, av: f.av || 0, recv: f.recv || 0, gstr: f.gstRecd || 0, tdsr: f.tdsRecorded || 0, tdsGap: f.tdsGap || 0, out: f.sold && f.av > 0 ? f.out : 0, pct: f.sold && f.av > 0 ? f.pctOut : 0, flags: f.sold ? f.flags.length : 0, sold: f.sold }));
  let list = rows.filter(r => {
    if (UI.filter === 'missingav' && !(r.sold&&r.av<=0)) return false;
    if (UI.filter === 'sold' && !r.sold) return false; if (UI.filter === 'unsold' && r.sold) return false;
    if (UI.filter === 'out' && !(r.out > 0)) return false; if (UI.filter === 'flags' && !(r.f.red > 0)) return false;
    if (UI.q && !(r.flat + ' ' + r.cust).toLowerCase().includes(UI.q.toLowerCase())) return false; return true;
  });
  const key = UI.sortKey, dir = UI.sortDir;
  list.sort((a, b) => { const kk = key === 'flat' ? 'num' : key; const x = a[kk], y = b[kk]; return (typeof x === 'string' ? x.localeCompare(y, undefined, { numeric: true }) : x - y) * dir; });
  const tot = list.reduce((t, r) => { t.area += r.area; t.av += r.av; t.recv += r.recv; t.out += r.out; if (r.sold && r.av > 0) t.areaB += r.area; return t; }, { area: 0, av: 0, recv: 0, out: 0, areaB: 0 });
  const th = (k, label, right) => '<th class="sortable ' + (right ? 'r' : '') + '" data-act="sort" data-k="' + k + '">' + label + (UI.sortKey === k ? (UI.sortDir > 0 ? ' &#9650;' : ' &#9660;') : '') + '</th>';
  let h = '<h2 class="pg">Sales &amp; Inventory</h2><p class="lead">Flat-wise summary: which flat is sold, at what rate, the agreement value, the amount outstanding and the percentage outstanding. Click a row to open that flat\'s dashboard.</p>';
  h += agreementCard() + inventoryCategoryPanels();
  h += '<div class="tools"><div class="seg">' + [['all', 'All flats'], ['sold', 'Sold / Booked'], ['missingav','Booked — agreement pending'], ['unsold', 'Unsold'], ['out', 'With outstanding'], ['flags', 'Red flags']].map(([k, l]) => '<button data-act="filter" data-k="' + k + '" aria-pressed="' + (UI.filter === k) + '">' + l + '</button>').join('') + '</div><input type="search" id="q" style="width:230px" placeholder="Search flat or customer" value="' + esc(UI.q) + '" aria-label="Search"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="exportSales" style="margin-left:auto">Download Excel</button></div>';
  if (UI.filter === 'unsold') { h += '<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Sr</th>' + th('flat', 'Flat No') + '<th>Floor</th>' + th('area', 'Area (sq ft)', 1) + '<th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + list.map((r, i) => '<tr class="click" data-act="openFlat" data-flat="' + r.norm + '"><td>' + (i + 1) + '</td><td><b>' + esc(r.flat) + '</b></td><td>' + (r.f.unit ? r.f.unit.floor : '') + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(r.area) + '</td><td>' + pill('brass', 'Unsold') + '</td></tr>').join('') + '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td></td><td>Total</td><td>' + list.length + ' flats</td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.area) + '</td><td></td></tr></tfoot></table></div>'; return h; }
  h += '<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Sr</th>' + th('flat', 'Flat No') + th('cust', 'Customer') + th('area', 'Area (sq ft)', 1) + th('rate', 'Rate (&#8377;/sq ft)', 1) + th('av', 'Agreement executed value (&#8377;)', 1) + th('recv', 'Received (&#8377;)', 1) + th('gstr', 'GST received (&#8377;)', 1) + th('tdsr', 'TDS received s.393 (&#8377;)', 1) + th('out', 'Outstanding (&#8377;)', 1) + th('pct', '% Outstanding', 1) + '<th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
    + list.map((r, i) => '<tr class="click ' + (r.sold ? '' : 'dim') + '" data-act="openFlat" data-flat="' + r.norm + '"><td>' + (i + 1) + '</td><td><b>' + esc(r.flat) + '</b></td><td>' + esc(r.cust) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(r.area) + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.rate ? inr(r.rate) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.sold ? (r.av ? inr(r.av) : '<span style="color:var(--violet)">Agreement pending</span>') : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.sold ? inr(r.recv) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.sold ? inr(r.gstr) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.sold && r.av ? inr(r.tdsr) + (r.tdsGap > 1000 ? '<div style="color:var(--red);font-size:11px;font-weight:600">short ' + inr(r.tdsGap) + '</div>' : '') : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.sold && r.av ? inr(r.out) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.sold && r.av ? '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end"><div class="hbar" style="width:54px"><i style="width:' + Math.min(100, r.pct * 5) + '%;background:' + (r.pct > 12 ? 'var(--red)' : r.pct > 8 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--teal)') + '"></i></div>' + pct1(r.pct) + '</div>' : '&mdash;') + '</td><td>' + (!r.sold ? pill('brass', 'Unsold') : r.f.status === 'booked' ? pill('violet', 'Agreement pending') : r.f.red ? pill('red', r.f.red + ' red flag' + (r.f.red > 1 ? 's' : '')) : r.f.amber ? pill('amber', 'Review') : pill('ok', 'Clear')) + '</td></tr>').join('')
    + '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td></td><td>Total</td><td>' + list.length + ' flats</td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.area) + '</td><td class="r">' + (tot.areaB ? inr(list.filter(r => r.sold && r.av > 0).reduce((x, r) => x + r.av, 0) / tot.areaB) : '') + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.av) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.recv) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(list.reduce((x, r) => x + (r.sold ? r.gstr : 0), 0)) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(list.reduce((x, r) => x + (r.sold ? r.tdsr : 0), 0)) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.out) + '</td><td class="r">' + (tot.av ? pct1(list.filter(r => r.sold && r.av > 0).reduce((x, r) => x + r.out, 0) / list.filter(r => r.sold && r.av > 0).reduce((x, r) => x + r.av, 0) * 100) : '') + '</td><td></td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  h += '<div class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">Rate in the total row is the weighted average of booked flats. Booked flats with receipts but no recorded sale value are shown as "AV missing" and excluded from outstanding. TDS: 1% buyer TDS where agreement value is ₹50 lakh or more — Income-tax Act 2025 s.393(1) (earlier s.194-IA); "short" = not recorded in the sales ledger.</div>';
  return h;
}
function exportSales() {
  const rows = [['Sr', 'Flat No', 'Customer', 'Area (sq ft)', 'Rate (Rs/sq ft)', 'Agreement Value', 'Received', 'GST received', 'TDS received (s.393)', 'TDS short', 'Outstanding', '% Outstanding']];
  let i = 0; T.list.filter(f => (f.unit && f.unit.saleable) || f.sold).forEach(f => { i++; rows.push([i, flatLabel(f.norm), f.sale ? f.sale.customer : 'Unsold', f.area, f.rate ? Math.round(f.rate) : '', f.sold ? f.av : '', f.sold ? Math.round(f.recv) : '', f.sold ? Math.round(f.gstRecd || 0) : '', f.sold ? Math.round(f.tdsRecorded || 0) : '', f.sold ? Math.round(f.tdsGap || 0) : '', f.sold && f.av > 0 ? Math.round(f.out) : '', f.sold && f.av > 0 ? +(f.pctOut.toFixed(2)) : '']); });
  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new(); XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rows), 'Sales & Inventory'); XLSX.writeFile(wb, 'Sales_and_Inventory_' + iso(T.ctx.asOf) + '.xlsx');
}

/* ---------- PROJECT & UNIT ---------- */
function vUnits() {
  const un = T.list.filter(f => f.unit && f.unit.saleable && !f.sold);
  const all = S.units.slice().sort((a, b) => +a.no - +b.no);
  let h = '<h2 class="pg">Project &amp; Unit</h2>';
  h += '<div class="tools"><div class="seg"><button data-act="units" data-k="inv" aria-pressed="' + !UI.showAll + '">Inventory (unsold)</button><button data-act="units" data-k="all" aria-pressed="' + UI.showAll + '">All units</button></div></div>';
  if (!UI.showAll) {
    h += '<div class="card" style="max-width:560px"><h3 style="font-size:20px">Inventory</h3><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Sr No.</th><th>Unit No</th><th class="r">Sq Ft</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + un.map((f, i) => '<tr><td>' + (i + 1) + '</td><td><b>' + esc(f.unit.no) + '</b></td><td class="r">' + inr(f.area) + '</td></tr>').join('') + '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td></td><td>Total</td><td class="r">' + inr(un.reduce((x, f) => x + f.area, 0)) + '</td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  } else {
    h += '<div class="card" style="max-width:680px"><h3 style="font-size:20px">All units</h3><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Sr No.</th><th>Unit No</th><th class="r">Sq Ft</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + all.map((u, i) => { const f = T.flats.get(u.norm); return '<tr class="' + (u.saleable ? '' : 'dim') + '"><td>' + (i + 1) + '</td><td><b>' + esc(u.no) + '</b></td><td class="r">' + (u.saleable ? inr(u.area) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td>' + (!u.saleable ? pill('grey', 'Refuge area') : f.sold ? pill('ok', 'Sold') : pill('brass', 'Unsold')) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td></td><td>Total saleable</td><td class="r">' + inr(T.areaTotal) + '</td><td>' + T.saleable + ' units</td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  }
  return h;
}

/* ---------- COMPANY PAYMENT SCHEDULE ---------- */
function vCoPlan() {
  const tot = S.schedule.reduce((x, m) => x + m.pct, 0);
  let h = '<h2 class="pg">Company Payment Demand Schedule</h2><p class="lead">The Company\'s standard construction-linked schedule (plan CLP-STD). Mark a milestone as achieved and enter its date, and every dashboard, overdue flag and demand letter updates. Each customer\'s own schedule is read from the Agreement for Sale; see Customer Payment Schedule.</p>';
  h += '<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Sr</th><th>Milestone</th><th class="r">% of agreement value</th><th class="r">Cumulative %</th><th>Achieved</th><th>Date achieved</th><th>Demand raised</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  let cum = 0;
  h += S.schedule.map((m, i) => { cum += m.pct; return '<tr><td>' + m.n + '</td><td>' + esc(m.name) + '</td><td class="r"><input type="number" step="0.5" min="0" max="100" value="' + +(m.pct * 100).toFixed(2) + '" data-sch="pct" data-i="' + i + '" aria-label="Percent for milestone ' + m.n + '"> %</td><td class="r"><b>' + +(cum * 100).toFixed(2) + '%</b></td><td><input type="checkbox" data-sch="ach" data-i="' + i + '"' + (m.achieved ? ' checked' : '') + ' aria-label="Achieved"></td><td><input type="date" data-sch="date" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + iso(m.date) + '"></td><td><input type="checkbox" data-sch="dem" data-i="' + i + '"' + (m.demandRaised ? ' checked' : '') + ' aria-label="Demand raised"> ' + (()=>{if(!m.achieved||!T)return '';const W=T.sold.filter(f=>f.av>0);let nT=0,nD=0;W.forEach(f=>{const r=f.rows[i];if(r&&r.amt>0.5){nT++;if(r.raised)nD++;}});return nD&&nD<nT?pill('amber','Partly · '+nD+' of '+nT):(nD&&nD===nT?pill('ok','All '+nT):'');})() + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
  h += '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td></td><td>Total</td><td class="r ' + (Math.abs(tot - 1) > 0.0001 ? 'bad' : 'ok') + '">' + +(tot * 100).toFixed(2) + '%</td><td></td><td colspan="3">' + (Math.abs(tot - 1) > 0.0001 ? 'Schedule must add up to 100%' : 'Adds up to 100%') + '</td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  h += '<div class="note info">Where do customer schedules come from? Each customer pays as per the Payment Schedule in his or her registered Agreement for Sale, so the contractual schedule is read from the agreement. Most customers follow this Company schedule; a customer with a different plan is handled in <a href="#" data-act="goTab" data-tab="custplan">Customer Payment Schedule</a>. ' + refPill('AGR-PAYMENT', '') + '</div>';
  return h;
}

/* ---------- CUSTOMER PAYMENT SCHEDULE ---------- */
function vCustPlan() {
  const list = T.list.filter(f => f.sold && f.av > 0);
  if (!list.find(f => f.norm === UI.custFlat)) UI.custFlat = list[0].norm;
  const f = T.flats.get(UI.custFlat); const cm = f.cust;
  const custom = S.custSchedules.has(UI.custFlat);
  const tot = f.rows.reduce((x, r) => ({ amt: x.amt + r.amt, paid: x.paid + r.paid, unpaid: x.unpaid + r.unpaid, int: x.int + r.interest }), { amt: 0, paid: 0, unpaid: 0, int: 0 });
  let h = '<h2 class="pg">Customer Payment Schedule</h2><p class="lead">This customer\'s payment schedule as per the Agreement for Sale, with what is paid and what is due. Amounts are agreement value × the stage percentage; receipts are applied to the earliest stage first.</p>';
  h += '<div class="sel">' + flatSelect('selCust', 'custFlat', list, UI.custFlat) + '<button class="btn line sm" data-act="' + (UI.editSched ? 'saveSched' : 'editSched') + '">' + (UI.editSched ? 'Save schedule' : 'Edit as per agreement') + '</button>' + (UI.editSched || custom ? '<button class="btn line sm" data-act="resetSched">Use Company schedule</button>' : '') + '</div>';
  h += '<div class="note info"><b>Source:</b> Agreement for Sale' + (cm.agreementDate ? ' dated ' + fmtDate(cm.agreementDate) : '') + (cm.regNo ? ' · Reg. No. ' + esc(cm.regNo) : '') + ' · Plan ' + esc(cm.plan || 'CLP-STD') + (custom ? ' <b>(customised for this customer)</b>' : ' (same as the Company schedule)') + ' &nbsp; ' + refPill('AGR-PAYMENT', f.norm) + '</div>';
  if (f.flags.length) h += '<div style="margin-bottom:12px">' + f.flags.filter(g => g.sev === 'red').map(g => '<div class="flag '+flagTone(g.code,g.sev)+'"><div class="ic">!</div><div style="flex:1"><div class="tt">' + esc(g.title) + '</div><div class="dd">' + esc(g.detail) + '</div>' + refs(g.refs, f.norm) + '</div></div>').join('') + '</div>';
  h += '<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Sr</th><th>Milestone</th><th class="r">%</th><th class="r">Amount (&#8377;)</th><th class="r">Cumulative (&#8377;)</th><th>Construction</th><th>Due date</th><th class="r">Paid (&#8377;)</th><th class="r">Balance (&#8377;)</th><th>Status</th><th class="r">Interest (&#8377;)</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
    + f.rows.map((r, i) => '<tr><td>' + r.n + '</td><td>' + esc(r.name) + '</td><td class="r">' + (UI.editSched ? '<input type="number" step="0.5" min="0" data-cs="' + i + '" value="' + +(r.pct * 100).toFixed(2) + '" aria-label="Percent">' : +(r.pct * 100).toFixed(2) + '%') + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(r.amt) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(r.cumDue) + '</td><td>' + (r.achieved ? '<span class="ok">&#10003; ' + fmtDate(r.date) + '</span>' : '<span class="hint">Pending</span>') + '</td><td>' + (r.dueDate ? fmtDate(r.dueDate) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(r.paid) + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.unpaid > 1 ? inr(r.unpaid) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td><span class="st ' + stCls(r.status) + '">' + r.status + '</span>' + (r.status === 'Overdue' ? ' <span class="hint">' + r.days + ' days</span>' : '') + '</td><td class="r">' + (r.interest ? inr(r.interest) : '&mdash;') + '</td></tr>').join('')
    + '</tbody><tfoot><tr><td></td><td>Total</td><td class="r">' + +(f.rows.reduce((x, r) => x + r.pct, 0) * 100).toFixed(2) + '%</td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.amt) + '</td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.paid) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.unpaid) + '</td><td></td><td class="r">' + inr(tot.int) + '</td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  h += '<div class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">Interest is indicative: simple interest at ' + T.ctx.c.rate + '% p.a. on the unpaid part of each overdue stage from its due date to ' + fmtDate(T.ctx.asOf) + '. Due date = demand date + ' + T.ctx.c.dueDays + ' days. ' + refPill('RERA-19-7', f.norm) + ' ' + refPill('RULE-18', f.norm) + '</div>';
  return h;
}

/* ---------- RECEIPTS & PAYMENTS ---------- */
const CREDIT_KINDS = [['demand_payment', 'Demand receipt incl. GST'], ['instalment', 'Instalment'], ['stamp', 'Stamp duty'], ['gst', 'GST'], ['transfer_in', 'Own-account transfer'], ['bank_interest', 'Bank interest / other']];
function vBank() {
  const tx = S.txns; const rec = tx.filter(t => t.cr > 0), pay = tx.filter(t => t.dr > 0);
  const totR = rec.reduce((x, t) => x + t.cr, 0), totP = pay.reduce((x, t) => x + t.dr, 0);
  const matched = rec.filter(t => t.flat).length, unm = rec.filter(t => t.kind === 'unmatched').length;
  const R = T.rera; const soldFlats = T.list.filter(f => f.sold);
  const heads = S.cost.map(c => c.head);
  let h = '<h2 class="pg">Receipts &amp; Payments</h2><p class="lead">Upload a bank statement (PDF, CSV or Excel). Entries are read, receipts are matched to flats from the narration, and the dashboards, overdue flags and cost figures update at once.</p>';
  h += '<div class="dz" id="dz" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Upload bank statement"><b>Drop a bank statement here, or click to choose</b><div class="hint" style="margin-top:4px">PDF, CSV or Excel · you can add several files · nothing leaves your computer</div></div>';
  if(S.files.length){h+='<div class="card" style="margin:12px 0"><h3>Statements uploaded <small>'+S.files.length+' file'+(S.files.length>1?'s':'')+' · '+tx.length+' entries</small></h3><div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>File</th><th class="r">Entries</th><th class="r">Receipts &#8377;</th><th class="r">Payments &#8377;</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+S.files.map((fn,i)=>{const q=S.txns.filter(t=>t.file===fn);return '<tr><td><b>'+esc(fn)+'</b></td><td class="r">'+q.length+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(q.reduce((x,t)=>x+(t.cr||0),0))+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(q.reduce((x,t)=>x+(t.dr||0),0))+'</td><td class="r"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="rmStmt" data-i="'+i+'">Remove this file</button></td></tr>';}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div><div class="tools" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="clearStmt">Remove all statements</button><span class="hint">Entries that were skipped as duplicates of an earlier file stay with the first file.</span></div></div>';}
  if (UI.report) h += '<div class="note ' + (UI.report.err ? 'bad' : 'ok') + '">' + UI.report.msg + '</div>';
  if (!tx.length) { h += '<div class="empty card" style="margin-top:12px">No statement loaded yet. Dashboards currently use the sales ledger as on ' + fmtDate(S.asOfBase) + '.</div>'; return h; }
  h += '<div class="grid g12" style="margin:14px 0"><div class="card kpi s3"><div class="lab">RECEIPTS</div><div class="big">' + cr(totR) + '</div><div class="sm">' + rec.length + ' entries · ' + matched + ' matched to flats</div></div><div class="card kpi s3"><div class="lab">PAYMENTS</div><div class="big">' + cr(totP) + '</div><div class="sm">' + pay.length + ' entries</div></div><div class="card kpi s3"><div class="lab">STATEMENT FILES</div><div class="big">' + S.files.length + '</div><div class="sm">' + esc(S.files.join(', ')).slice(0, 80) + '</div></div><div class="card kpi s3"><div class="lab">NEEDS ATTENTION</div><div class="big ' + (unm ? 'warn' : 'ok') + '">' + unm + '</div><div class="sm">unmatched receipt(s) to assign</div></div></div>';
  h += '<div class="tools"><div class="seg"><button data-act="rcp" data-k="receipts" aria-pressed="' + (UI.rcp === 'receipts') + '">Receipts (' + rec.length + ')</button><button data-act="rcp" data-k="payments" aria-pressed="' + (UI.rcp === 'payments') + '">Payments (' + pay.length + ')</button></div></div>';
  if (UI.rcp === 'receipts') {
    if (unm) h += '<div class="tools" style="margin-bottom:8px"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="aiMatchAll">AI suggest for all unmatched (' + unm + ')</button><span class="hint">AI only suggests. Nothing is applied until you click Accept.</span></div>';
    h += '<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Narration</th><th class="r">Amount (&#8377;)</th><th>Applied to flat</th><th>Type</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + rec.map(t => '<tr><td>' + fmtDate(t.date) + '</td><td>' + esc(t.narr) + (t.matchBy ? '<div class="hint">matched by ' + esc(t.matchBy) + '</div>' : '') + aiMatchCell(t) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(t.cr) + '</td><td>' + (t.kind === 'transfer_in' || t.kind === 'bank_interest' ? '<span class="hint">not a customer receipt</span>' : '<select data-tx="flat" data-id="' + t.id + '" aria-label="Flat"><option value="">— assign flat —</option>' + soldFlats.map(f => '<option value="' + f.norm + '"' + (t.flat === f.norm ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(flatLabel(f.norm)) + ' · ' + esc(f.sale.customer) + '</option>').join('') + '</select>') + '</td><td>' + (t.kind === 'unmatched' ? pill('amber', 'Unmatched') : '<select data-tx="kind" data-id="' + t.id + '" aria-label="Type">' + CREDIT_KINDS.map(([k, l]) => '<option value="' + k + '"' + (t.kind === k ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + l + '</option>').join('') + '</select>') + '</td></tr>').join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
  } else {
    const cats = [...heads, 'Taxes & duties (not project cost)', 'Inter-account transfer', 'Cheque return / bank charges', 'Uncategorised'];
    const curCat = t => t.kind === 'cost' ? t.cat : t.kind === 'tax_paid' ? 'Taxes & duties (not project cost)' : (t.kind === 'transfer_rera' || t.kind === 'transfer_free') ? 'Inter-account transfer' : (t.kind === 'chq_return' || t.kind === 'bank_charges') ? 'Cheque return / bank charges' : 'Uncategorised';
    h += '<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Narration</th><th class="r">Amount (&#8377;)</th><th>Category</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + pay.map(t => '<tr><td>' + fmtDate(t.date) + '</td><td>' + esc(t.narr) + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(t.dr) + '</td><td>' + ((t.kind === 'chq_return' || t.kind === 'bank_charges') ? '<span class="hint">Cheque return / bank charges' + (t.flat ? ' · Flat ' + esc(flatLabel(t.flat)) : '') + '</span>' : '<select data-tx="cat" data-id="' + t.id + '" aria-label="Category">' + cats.map(c => '<option' + (curCat(t) === c ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(c) + '</option>').join('') + '</select>') + '</td></tr>').join('') + '</tbody></table></div><div class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">Payments are cash-flow data only. Update incurred cost from project books in the Project Cost Excel sheet; bank payments are not added again.</div>';
  }
  return h;
}
async function ingestFile(name, kind, data) {
  let txns = [];
  if (kind === 'pdf') {
    if (typeof pdfjsLib === 'undefined') throw new Error('PDF reader is not available.');
    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = SLAB_PDF_WORKER_URL;
    let pw;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
      try { const lines = await pdfToLines(pdfjsLib, new Uint8Array(data), pw); txns = parsePdfLines(lines); break; }
      catch (e) { if (e && e.name === 'PasswordException') { pw = prompt('This PDF is password protected. Enter the password:'); if (!pw) throw new Error('Password needed for ' + name); } else throw e; }
    }
  } else {
    const wb = kind === 'csvtext' ? XLSX.read(data, { type: 'string', raw: true }) : XLSX.read(data, { type: 'array', cellDates: true, raw: true });
    const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]], { header: 1, defval: null, raw: true });
    const r = parseCSVRows(rows); if (r.error) throw new Error(r.error); txns = r.txns;
  }
  if (!txns.length) throw new Error('No transactions found in ' + name + '. Check that it is a bank statement with date, narration, debit and credit columns.');
  const res = addTxns(S, txns, name);
  return { name, n: txns.length, added: res.added, dup: res.dup };
}
async function ingestMany(items) {
  UI.report = null; let msgs = [], err = false;
  for (const it of items) {
    try { const r = await ingestFile(it.name, it.kind, it.data); msgs.push('<b>' + esc(r.name) + '</b>: ' + r.added + ' entries read' + (r.dup ? ', ' + r.dup + ' already loaded' : '')); }
    catch (e) { err = true; msgs.push('<b>' + esc(it.name) + '</b>: ' + esc(e.message || e)); }
  }
  UI.report = { err, msg: msgs.join('<br>') }; recompute(); render();
  if (!err) toast('Statement read. Dashboards updated.');
}
function b64ToBytes(b64) { const bin = atob(b64); const a = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) a[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i); return a; }
function handleFiles(files) {
  const items = []; let pending = files.length;
  [...files].forEach(f => {
    const ext = f.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase(); const rd = new FileReader();
    rd.onload = () => { items.push({ name: f.name, kind: ext === 'pdf' ? 'pdf' : 'xl', data: rd.result }); if (--pending === 0) ingestMany(items); };
    rd.readAsArrayBuffer(f);
  });
}

' checked' : '') + ' aria-label="Select flat ' + esc(d.label) + '"></td><td><b>' + esc(d.label) + '</b></td><td>' + esc(d.customer) + '</td><td style="font-size:12px">' + esc(d.email) + (TEST_RX.test(d.email) ? ' ' + pill('ok', 'test inbox') : '') + '</td><td class="r">' + inr(d.inst) + '</td><td class="r">' + (d.arrears ? inr(d.arrears) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (d.interest ? inr(d.interest) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (d.gst ? inr(d.gst) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r">' + (d.sd ? inr(d.sd) : '&mdash;') + '</td><td class="r"><b>' + inr(d.total) + '</b></td></tr>').join('')

'' : ' disabled') + '>' + (UI.busy ? 'Sending…' : (SET.dry ? 'Raise demand for ' : 'Raise demand and send emails for ') + sel.length + ' customer' + (sel.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + (SET.dry ? ' (test run, no email)' : '')) + '</button><span class="hint">Total demand ' + rs(selTot) + '</span></div>';
  if (UI.last) h += UI.last;
  h += '</div><div class="card s12"><h3>Email and invoice preview <small>click a row above to preview</small></h3>' + (UI.prev && rows.find(d => d.flat === UI.prev) ? (()=>{const PL=letterModel(S, T, rows.find(d => d.flat === UI.prev), 1, todayDate());return '<div style="font-weight:700;margin:4px 0 6px">Email body (short, formal)</div><div style="border:1px solid #d8dee6;border-radius:10px;padding:14px;background:#fff;max-width:820px;margin:0 auto 14px">'+demandEmailHtml(PL)+'</div><div style="font-weight:700;margin:4px 0 6px">Attachment: tax invoice-cum-demand (PDF) · same layout as below</div><div class="paper" style="max-width:820px;margin:0 auto">' + letterHtml(PL) + '</div>';})() + '<div style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="pdfOne" data-flat="' + UI.prev + '">Download this letter (PDF)</button></div>' : '<div class="empty">Select a row to preview the letter on the Company letterhead.</div>') + '</div></div>';
  return h;
}
function buildLetters(rows) {
  const today = todayDate();
  return rows.map((d, i) => { const L = letterModel(S, T, d, S.demands.length + i + 1, today); return { d, L }; });
}
function letterPdf(L){
 const {jsPDF}=window.jspdf,doc=new jsPDF({unit:'mm',format:'a4',compress:true}),M=12,W=186,X2=M+W,D=invoiceDemandData(L);
 const safe=s=>String(s??'').replace(/₹/g,'Rs. ').replace(/[–—−]/g,'-').replace(/[“”]/g,'"').replace(/[‘’]/g,"'").replace(/·/g,'|');
 const f=(sz,b)=>{doc.setFont('helvetica',b?'bold':'normal');doc.setFontSize(sz);};
 const ink=()=>doc.setTextColor(23,34,45),grey=()=>doc.setTextColor(93,107,122);
 const lh=sz=>sz*0.3528*1.3;
 const wrap=(t,w,sz,b)=>{f(sz,b);return doc.splitTextToSize(safe(t),w);};
 const hline=yy=>doc.line(M,yy,X2,yy);
 doc.setDrawColor(23,34,45);doc.setLineWidth(0.3);
 let y=M;
 /* header */
 const nl=wrap(L.co.name.toUpperCase(),120,13,true),al=wrap((L.co.l1+' '+L.co.l2).trim(),120,7.5),il=wrap(D.ids,120,7.5);
 let ty=y+7;f(13,true);ink();nl.forEach(t=>{doc.text(t,M+3,ty);ty+=lh(13);});
 f(7.5);grey();al.concat(il).forEach(t=>{doc.text(t,M+3,ty);ty+=lh(7.5);});
 f(11,true);ink();doc.text('TAX INVOICE-CUM-DEMAND',X2-3,y+7,{align:'right'});
 f(7.5);grey();doc.text('Original for recipient',X2-3,y+11.5,{align:'right'});
 f(6.8,true);doc.setTextColor(165,40,40);doc.text('DEMONSTRATION: FICTIONAL DATA',X2-3,y+15.5,{align:'right'});
 y=Math.max(ty,y+18)+1.5;doc.setLineWidth(0.5);hline(y);doc.setLineWidth(0.3);
 /* two-column info rows */
 const cw=W/2;
 const cellH=(c,w)=>{let h=5;if(c.kv)c.kv.forEach(([k,v])=>{h+=Math.max(1,wrap(v,w-6-30,8).length)*lh(8);});if(c.lines)c.lines.forEach((l,i)=>{h+=wrap(l,w-6,i===0&&c.boldFirst?9:8,i===0&&c.boldFirst).length*lh(i===0&&c.boldFirst?9:8);});return h+3;};
 const drawCell=(c,x,yy,w)=>{f(6.5,true);grey();doc.text(c.title.toUpperCase(),x+3,yy+3.8);let cy=yy+7.5;
  if(c.kv)c.kv.forEach(([k,v])=>{f(7.5);grey();doc.text(safe(k),x+3,cy);const ls=wrap(v,w-6-30,8);f(8);ink();doc.text(ls,x+3+30,cy);cy+=ls.length*lh(8);});
  if(c.lines)c.lines.forEach((l,i)=>{const b=i===0&&c.boldFirst,sz=b?9:8,ls=wrap(l,w-6,sz,b);f(sz,b);ink();doc.text(ls,x+3,cy);cy+=ls.length*lh(sz);});};
 const row2=(a,b)=>{const h=Math.max(cellH(a,cw),cellH(b,cw));drawCell(a,M,y,cw);drawCell(b,M+cw,y,cw);doc.line(M+cw,y,M+cw,y+h);y+=h;hline(y);return h;};
 row2({title:'Invoice details',kv:D.invoice},{title:'Supply details',kv:D.supply});
 row2({title:'Bill to (allottee)',lines:D.bill,boldFirst:true},{title:'Agreement',lines:D.agr});
 /* items table */
 const nt=D.taxHead.length,tw=nt===2?[62,16,28,26,26,28]:[62,16,30,50,28];
 const cx=[M];tw.forEach(w=>cx.push(cx[cx.length-1]+w));
 const tTop=y,hh=7;doc.setFillColor(232,237,242);doc.rect(M,y,W,hh,'F');
 const heads=['Description of service','SAC','Taxable value (Rs.)',...D.taxHead.map(h=>h+' (Rs.)'),'Total (Rs.)'];
 f(7.2,true);ink();heads.forEach((t,i)=>{if(i<2)doc.text(t,cx[i]+2,y+4.6);else doc.text(t,cx[i+1]-2,y+4.6,{align:'right'});});
 y+=hh;hline(y);
 const num=n=>inr2(n);
 D.items.forEach(it=>{const dl=wrap(it.desc,tw[0]-4,8),h=Math.max(dl.length*lh(8),5)+3;f(8);ink();doc.text(dl,cx[0]+2,y+4.2);
  if(it.sac)doc.text(safe(it.sac),cx[1]+2,y+4.2);
  if(it.taxable!=null){doc.text(num(it.taxable),cx[3]-2,y+4.2,{align:'right'});it.tax.forEach((t,k)=>doc.text(num(t),cx[4+k]-2,y+4.2,{align:'right'}));}
  doc.text(num(it.total),cx[cx.length-1]-2,y+4.2,{align:'right'});y+=h;hline(y);});
 const sumTaxable=D.items.reduce((a,i)=>a+(i.taxable||0),0),sumTax=D.taxHead.map((_,k)=>D.items.filter(i=>i.tax).reduce((a,i)=>a+i.tax[k],0));
 doc.setFillColor(232,237,242);doc.rect(M,y,W,7,'F');f(8.2,true);ink();doc.text('Total amount payable',cx[0]+2,y+4.7);
 doc.text(num(sumTaxable),cx[3]-2,y+4.7,{align:'right'});sumTax.forEach((t,k)=>doc.text(num(t),cx[4+k]-2,y+4.7,{align:'right'}));doc.text(num(D.total),cx[cx.length-1]-2,y+4.7,{align:'right'});
 y+=7;
 for(let i=1;i<cx.length-1;i++)doc.line(cx[i],tTop,cx[i],y);
 hline(y);
 /* amount in words */
 const wl=wrap('Amount in words: '+D.words,W-6,8.5,true);f(8.5,true);ink();doc.text(wl,M+3,y+5);y+=wl.length*lh(8.5)+4.5;hline(y);
 /* account strip */
 const sw=W/4;D.strip.forEach(([k,v],i)=>{f(6.2,true);grey();doc.text(safe(k).toUpperCase(),M+i*sw+3,y+4,{maxWidth:sw-5});f(8.5,true);ink();doc.text(safe(v),M+i*sw+3,y+9);if(i<3)doc.line(M+(i+1)*sw,y,M+(i+1)*sw,y+12);});y+=12;hline(y);
 /* pay to + terms */
 const lw=cw,rw=W-cw,payH=(()=>{let h=7.5;D.bank.forEach(([k,v])=>{h+=Math.max(1,wrap(v,lw-6-30,8).length)*lh(8);});return h+2;})();
 const tl=D.terms.map((t,i)=>wrap((i+1)+'. '+t,rw-6,7.6)),termH=7.5+tl.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.length*lh(7.6)+0.8,0)+2,ph=Math.max(payH,termH);
 drawCell({title:'Pay to',kv:D.bank},M,y,lw);
 f(6.5,true);grey();doc.text('TERMS',M+lw+3,y+3.8);let tyy=y+7.5;f(7.6);ink();tl.forEach(l=>{doc.text(l,M+lw+3,tyy);tyy+=l.length*lh(7.6)+0.8;});
 doc.line(M+lw,y,M+lw,y+ph);y+=ph;hline(y);
 /* signature */
 const sh=30;f(8.5,true);ink();doc.text('For '+safe(L.co.name),X2-3,y+6,{align:'right'});
 f(8.5,true);doc.text(safe(D.sign[0]),X2-3,y+22,{align:'right'});f(7);grey();doc.text(safe(D.sign[1]),X2-3,y+26,{align:'right'});
 y+=sh;
 /* outer border and note */
 doc.setLineWidth(0.5);doc.rect(M,M,W,y-M);doc.setLineWidth(0.3);
 const nl2=wrap(D.note,W,6.8);f(6.8);grey();doc.text(nl2,M+W/2,y+4,{align:'center'});
 return doc;
}

const letterB64 = L => letterPdf(L).output('datauristring').split(',')[1];
function demandPayload(letters, batchId) {
  return { token: SET.token, batchId, source: 'Miles2Collect', project: infoGet(S, 'Project Name', ''), demands: letters.map(({ d, L }) => ({ flat: d.label, customer: d.customer, to: d.email, subject: 'Demand for milestone stage - ' + d.mName + ' - Flat ' + d.label, html: demandEmailHtml(L), pdfBase64: letterB64(L), fileName: 'Demo_Tax_Invoice_Flat_' + d.label + '.pdf', fromName: L.co.name, amount: Math.round(d.total), milestone: d.mName })) };
}
async function sendDemands() {
  if(!aiGateOk()){toast('Run the AI cross-check on the architect certificate first.',4500);return;}
  if(!approvalOk()){toast('Manager approval is required before demand emails.',4500);return;}
  const ms = UI.demandMs; const allEligible=T.sold.map(f=>demandFor(S,T,f.norm,ms)).filter(Boolean);const selectedAll=allEligible.length>0&&allEligible.every(d=>UI.sel&&UI.sel.has(d.flat));
  // raising = recording the selected customers' demands (email is a separate, demo-restricted step)
  {const pick=[...(UI.sel||[])];let n=0;S.demands.filter(d=>d.milestone===ms&&pick.includes(d.flat)).forEach(d=>{if(!d.issued){d.issued=true;n++;}if(!d.issuedOn)d.issuedOn=iso(todayDate());if(!d.ok)d.status='Raised · email pending';});
   if(selectedAll){const stage=S.schedule.find(m=>m.n===ms);if(stage)stage.demandRaised=true;flow().allStageRaised=true;}
   if(pick.length){recompute();}}
  const rows = [...(UI.sel||[])].filter(f=>!S.demands.some(d=>d.flat===f && d.milestone===ms && d.ok)).map(f => demandFor(S, T, f, ms)).filter(Boolean);
  const allowed=String(infoGet(S,'Demo Demand Flats','')).split(',').map(normFlat);
  const permitted=rows.filter(d=>allowed.includes(d.flat)&&TEST_RX.test(d.email));
  const excluded=rows.length-permitted.length;
  rows.splice(0,rows.length,...permitted);
  if (!rows.length) { persistProject();render();toast('Demand raised for the selected customers. Email goes only to the four demo inboxes; select one of them to send.',5000); return; }
  if(excluded)toast(excluded+' selected customer(s) excluded: demo sends only to the four configured recipients.',6000);
  UI.busy = true; render();
  try {
    const t = todayDate(); const batchId = 'DB-' + iso(t).replace(/-/g, '') + '-' + String(S.demands.filter((x, i, a) => a.findIndex(y => y.batchId === x.batchId) === i).length + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const payload = demandPayload(buildLetters(rows), batchId);
    payload.demands.forEach(d=>{d.reference='M2C-'+normFlat(d.flat)+'-M'+ms; d.idempotencyKey=d.reference+'-'+iso(toDate(flow().date)||t);});
    if (SET.dry) {
      const show = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(payload)); show.demands.forEach(x => { x.pdfBase64 = '(' + Math.round(x.pdfBase64.length * 0.75 / 1024) + ' KB PDF, base64)'; x.html = '(' + x.html.length + ' characters of HTML)'; }); show.token = '(hidden)';
      UI.last = '<div class="note info" style="margin-top:12px"><b>Dry run: nothing was sent.</b> This is what would be posted to n8n:<pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:11.5px;max-height:260px;overflow:auto;margin:8px 0 0">' + esc(JSON.stringify(show, null, 2)) + '</pre></div>';
    } else {
      const res = await fetch(SET.webhook, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) });
      let js = {}; try { js = await res.json(); } catch (e) { }
      if (!res.ok || !js.ok) throw new Error(res.status === 401 ? 'n8n rejected the token. Check the token in the connection settings.' : 'n8n replied with status ' + res.status + (js.message ? ': ' + js.message : ''));
      const queued = js.queued || [], skipped = js.skipped || [];
      rows.forEach(d => { const q = queued.includes(d.label), sent = (js.sent||[]).includes(d.label); recordDemandResult(d,t,batchId,sent?'Email sent':q?'Email sent':'Not confirmed by n8n',q||sent); });
      const accepted=rows.filter(d=>queued.includes(d.label)||(js.sent||[]).includes(d.label));
      if(!S.emailBatches)S.emailBatches=[];
      S.emailBatches.push({batchId,date:t,milestone:ms,requested:rows.map(d=>d.flat),accepted:accepted.map(d=>d.flat),selectedAll,deliveryVerified:false,note:'Demo purpose: email requested for only '+rows.length+' flat(s); n8n/Gmail accepted '+accepted.length+'. Delivery is not verified.'});
      if(selectedAll&&accepted.length===rows.length){
       S.demands.filter(d=>d.milestone===ms).forEach(d=>{d.issued=true;if(!d.ok)d.status='Raised locally · demo email not requested';});
       const stage=S.schedule.find(m=>m.n===ms);if(stage)stage.demandRaised=true;
       flow().allStageRaised=true;
      }
      UI.last = '<div class="note ok" style="margin-top:12px"><b>Demand raised.</b> n8n accepted batch ' + esc(batchId) + ': ' + queued.length + ' request(s) accepted' + (queued.length ? ' (' + queued.map(esc).join(', ') + ')' : '') + (skipped.length ? '. Skipped by the demo safety filter: ' + skipped.map(esc).join(', ') : '') + '.</div>';
      recompute(); toast('Demand raised for ' + queued.length + ' customer(s)');
    }
  } catch (e) {
    const net = /Failed to fetch|NetworkError|Load failed/i.test(String(e.message));
    UI.last = '<div class="note bad" style="margin-top:12px"><b>Could not complete.</b> ' + esc(net ? 'The browser could not reach n8n. Check your internet connection, that the workflow is published, and the webhook URL above.' : e.message || e) + '</div>';
  }
  UI.busy = false; render();
}

/* ===== AI narration matching (n8n + Gemini): suggestion only, user must Accept ===== */
function aiMatchCell(t){
  if(t.kind!=='unmatched'&&!(t.aiSug&&t.aiSug.accepted))return '';
  const a=t.aiSug;
  if(t.aiBusy)return '<div class="hint" style="margin-top:4px">AI is reading the narration…</div>';
  if(!a)return '<div style="margin-top:4px"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="aiMatch" data-id="'+t.id+'">AI suggest</button></div>';
  if(a.accepted)return '<div class="hint" style="margin-top:4px;color:#13795b">AI suggestion accepted: Flat '+esc(a.label||'')+'</div>';
  if(a.error)return '<div class="hint" style="margin-top:4px;color:#b42318">AI not available ('+esc(a.error)+'). Assign the flat manually.</div><button class="btn line sm" data-act="aiMatch" data-id="'+t.id+'">Retry</button>';
  if(!a.flat)return '<div class="note" style="margin:6px 0 0;padding:6px 10px;background:#fff7e6;border:1px solid #f0d28a;border-radius:8px;font-size:12.5px"><b>AI: no confident match.</b> '+esc(a.reason||'')+'<div style="margin-top:4px"><button class="btn line sm" data-act="aiIgnore" data-id="'+t.id+'">Dismiss</button> <span class="hint">Assign the flat manually.</span></div></div>';
  const low=a.conf<0.7;
  return '<div class="note" style="margin:6px 0 0;padding:6px 10px;background:'+(low?'#fff7e6':'#eef7f2')+';border:1px solid '+(low?'#f0d28a':'#bfe0cf')+';border-radius:8px;font-size:12.5px"><b>AI suggests:</b> Flat '+esc(a.label)+' · '+esc(a.customer)+' · confidence '+Math.round(a.conf*100)+'%'+(low?' <b>(low: review manually)</b>':'')+'<div style="margin-top:2px">'+esc(a.reason||'')+'</div><div style="margin-top:4px">'+(low?'':'<button class="btn sm" data-act="aiAccept" data-id="'+t.id+'">Accept</button> ')+'<button class="btn line sm" data-act="aiIgnore" data-id="'+t.id+'">Ignore</button></div></div>';
}
async function aiMatchOne(t,quiet){
  if(!SET.matchWebhook){if(!quiet)toast('Set the AI narration-matching webhook in Letterhead and n8n connection');return;}
  const sold=T.list.filter(f=>f.sold);
  const flats=sold.map(f=>({flat:flatLabel(f.norm),customer:f.sale.customer,openDemands:S.demands.filter(d=>d.flat===f.norm&&!S.txns.some(x=>x.demRef===d.ref)).map(d=>({ref:d.ref,amount:d.amount}))}));
  t.aiBusy=true;
  try{
    const body={action:'match_narration',token:SET.token,project:infoGet(S,'Project Name',''),narration:t.narr,amount:t.cr,date:t.date?iso(t.date):null,flats};
    const res=await fetch(SET.matchWebhook,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('n8n replied '+res.status);
    let j=await res.json();if(Array.isArray(j))j=j[0];if(j&&j.output)j=j.output;if(typeof j==='string')j=JSON.parse(j.replace(/^```(?:json)?|```$/g,''));
    let hit=null;
    if(j&&j.matched&&j.flat!=null){const k=normFlat(String(j.flat));hit=sold.find(f=>f.norm===k)||null;}
    const conf=j&&j.confidence!=null?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,+j.confidence)):0;
    if(hit)t.aiSug={flat:hit.norm,label:flatLabel(hit.norm),customer:hit.sale.customer,conf:conf,reason:String(j.reason||''),at:new Date().toISOString()};
    else t.aiSug={flat:null,conf:conf,reason:(j&&j.matched&&j.flat!=null)?'AI named a flat that is not in the sold list, so the answer was discarded.':String((j&&j.reason)||'No usable evidence in the narration.'),at:new Date().toISOString()};
  }catch(e){t.aiSug={error:/fetch/i.test(String(e.message))?'cannot reach n8n':String(e.message||e),at:new Date().toISOString()};}
  t.aiBusy=false;
}
async function pingN8n() {
  try { const res = await fetch(SET.webhook, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ token: SET.token, batchId: 'PING', demands: [] }) }); const js = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); if (res.ok && js.ok) toast('Connected. n8n accepted the token.'); else toast(res.status === 401 ? 'n8n reached, but the token was rejected.' : 'n8n replied with status ' + res.status); }
  catch (e) { toast('Could not reach n8n. Check the URL and your internet connection.', 4500); }
}

/* ---------- shell: render, events ---------- */
function render() {
  try { const _t = document.getElementById('tip'); if (_t) _t.classList.remove('on'); } catch (e) {}
  if(!S.loaded && !['master','sales','upmaster'].includes(UI.tab)) UI.tab=roleTabs().some(t=>t[0]==='upmaster')?'upmaster':(roleTabs().some(t=>t[0]==='master')?'master':'sales');
  if (UI.role && !roleTabs().some(t => t[0] === UI.tab)) UI.tab = ROLES[UI.role].home;
  $('#nav').innerHTML = roleTabs().map(([k, l]) => '<button class="tab" role="tab" data-act="goTab" data-tab="' + k + '" aria-selected="' + (UI.tab === k) + '">' + l + (k==='sched'&&UI.role==='manager'&&S.loaded&&approvalPending()?' <span style="color:#f0b429">● 1 pending</span>':'') + '</button>').join('') + (UI.role ? '<span style="margin-left:auto;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;color:#c9d6e3;font-size:12.5px;padding-right:6px">Signed in: <b style="color:#fff">' + esc(UI.user.name) + '</b> · ' + esc(ROLES[UI.role].label) + '<button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="logout">Switch user</button></span>' : '');
  if(!S.loaded && !['master','sales'].includes(UI.tab)) UI.tab=(UI.role&&!roleTabs().some(t=>t[0]==='master'))?'sales':'master';
  if (UI.role && !roleTabs().some(t => t[0] === UI.tab)) UI.tab = ROLES[UI.role].home;
  const f = {master:vDashboardFive,pnl:vPnl,sales:vSalesFive,work:vWorkFive,sched:vSchedFive,coll:vCollMgr,remind:vRemindTab,upmaster:vUpMaster,reports:vReports,bank:vBankFive}[UI.tab] || (UI.role && UI.role !== 'director' ? vSalesFive : vDashboardFive);
  const scroll = window.scrollY;
  $('#view').setAttribute('data-view',UI.tab);
  $('#view').innerHTML = (UI.role==='manager'&&S.loaded&&approvalPending()&&UI.tab!=='sched'?'<div class="note info" style="margin-bottom:14px;border-left:4px solid #bf9635"><b>Approval pending:</b> the architect certificate submitted by '+esc(flow().approval.submittedBy)+' is waiting for your decision. <button class="btn sm" data-act="goTab" data-tab="sched">Review and approve</button></div>':'') + f();
  $('#view').querySelectorAll('p.lead,p.hint').forEach(e=>e.remove());
  const nS = S.files.length;
  $('#chipData').innerHTML = !S.loaded ? 'Upload master data to begin' : '<i></i>Sales ledger ' + fmtDate(S.asOfBase) + (nS ? ' · ' + nS + ' bank statement' + (nS > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' to ' + fmtDate(T.ctx.asOf) : '');
  if (UI.tab === 'bank') { const dz = $('#dz'); if (dz) { dz.onclick = () => $('#fileStmt').click(); dz.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); $('#fileStmt').click(); } }; ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach(ev => dz.addEventListener(ev, e => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.add('over'); })); ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(ev => dz.addEventListener(ev, e => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.remove('over'); })); dz.addEventListener('drop', e => { if (e.dataTransfer.files.length) handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files); }); } }
  if (UI.keepScroll) { window.scrollTo(0, scroll); UI.keepScroll = false; }
}
function goTab(t) { if(['flat','custplan','units'].includes(t)){UI.salesDetail=t;t='sales';} if(t==='coplan')t='sched';if(t==='demand')t='work';if(t==='flow')t='master';if(UI.role==='accountant'&&t==='sales')t='upmaster';if(UI.role&&!roleTabs().some(x=>x[0]===t)){toast('Not available for the '+ROLES[UI.role].label+' role');return;}UI.tab = t; UI.last = null; closeDrawer(); render(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); }
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-act]'); if (!el) return;
  const a = el.dataset.act;
  if (el.tagName === 'A') e.preventDefault();
  if (el.tagName === 'TR' && e.target.closest('input,select,a,button')) { if (!(e.target.closest('a') && el.dataset.act === 'openFlat')) { if (e.target.closest('input,select')) return; } }
  switch (a) {
    case 'goTab': if(el.dataset.tab==='sales')UI.salesDetail=null;goTab(el.dataset.tab); break;
    case 'whatsappDraft':whatsappDraft(el.dataset.flat);break;
    case 'reportExcel':downloadManagementExcel();break;
    case 'reportPdf':downloadManagementReport();break;
    case 'reportEmail':emailManagementReport();break;
    case 'salesList':UI.salesDetail=null;goTab('sales');break;
    case 'upload': $('#fileXlsx').click(); break;
    case 'ref': openRef(el.dataset.ref, el.dataset.flat); break;
    case 'closeDrawer': closeDrawer(); break;
    case 'tile': { const f = T.flats.get(el.dataset.flat); if (f && (f.sold || (f.unit && f.unit.saleable))) { UI.flat = el.dataset.flat; goTab('flat'); } break; }
    case 'openFlat': UI.flat = el.dataset.flat; UI.custFlat = el.dataset.flat; goTab('flat'); break;
    case 'goCust': UI.custFlat = el.dataset.flat; goTab('custplan'); break;
    case 'prevFlat': case 'nextFlat': { const list = [...T.list.filter(f => f.sold), ...T.list.filter(f => !f.sold && f.unit && f.unit.saleable)]; let i = list.findIndex(f => f.norm === UI.flat); i = (i + (a === 'nextFlat' ? 1 : -1) + list.length) % list.length; UI.flat = list[i].norm; render(); break; }
    case 'sort': if (UI.sortKey === el.dataset.k) UI.sortDir *= -1; else { UI.sortKey = el.dataset.k; UI.sortDir = 1; } render(); break;
    case 'filter': UI.filter = el.dataset.k; render(); break;
    case 'units': UI.showAll = el.dataset.k === 'all'; render(); break;
    case 'exportSales': exportSales(); break;
    case 'editSched': UI.editSched = true; render(); break;
    case 'saveSched': { const inputs = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-cs]')]; const p = inputs.map(i => (parseFloat(i.value) || 0) / 100); const sum = p.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0); if (Math.abs(sum - 1) > 0.0001) { toast('Percentages add up to ' + +(sum * 100).toFixed(2) + '%. They must add up to 100%.', 4200); break; } S.custSchedules.set(UI.custFlat, p); UI.editSched = false; recompute(); render(); toast('Schedule saved for this customer'); break; }
    case 'resetSched': S.custSchedules.delete(UI.custFlat); UI.editSched = false; recompute(); render(); break;
    case 'rcp': UI.rcp = el.dataset.k; render(); break;
    case 'sampleStmt': { const s = SAMPLE_STMT[+el.dataset.i]; ingestMany([{ name: s.name, kind: s.kind === 'pdf' ? 'pdf' : 'csvtext', data: s.kind === 'pdf' ? b64ToBytes(s.b64).buffer : s.text }]); break; }
    case 'rmStmt': {const fn=S.files[+el.dataset.i];if(fn!=null){S.txns=S.txns.filter(t=>t.file!==fn);S.files=S.files.filter(f=>f!==fn);UI.report=null;recompute();render();toast('Removed '+fn);}} break;
    case 'clearStmt': S.txns = []; S.files = []; UI.report = null; recompute(); render(); toast('Statements removed'); break;
    case 'prevLetter': UI.prev = el.dataset.flat; UI.keepScroll = true; render(); break;
    case 'selTest': { const rows = T.list.filter(f => f.sold).map(f => demandFor(S, T, f.norm, UI.demandMs)).filter(Boolean); UI.sel = new Set(rows.filter(d => TEST_RX.test(d.email)).map(d => d.flat)); render(); break; }
    case 'selAll': { const rows = T.list.filter(f => f.sold).map(f => demandFor(S, T, f.norm, UI.demandMs)).filter(Boolean); UI.sel = new Set(rows.map(d => d.flat)); render(); break; }
    case 'selNone': UI.sel = new Set(); render(); break;
    case 'send': sendDemands(); break;
    case 'aiMatch': { const t=S.txns.find(x=>x.id===+el.dataset.id); if(t){ UI.keepScroll=true; t.aiBusy=true; render(); aiMatchOne(t).then(()=>{ persistProject(); UI.keepScroll=true; render(); }); } break; }
    case 'aiMatchAll': { const list=S.txns.filter(x=>x.cr>0&&x.kind==='unmatched'&&!x.aiSug); if(!list.length){toast('Nothing left to suggest');break;} UI.keepScroll=true; list.forEach(t=>t.aiBusy=true); render(); Promise.all(list.map(t=>aiMatchOne(t,true))).then(()=>{ persistProject(); UI.keepScroll=true; render(); toast('AI suggestions ready. Review each one before accepting.',4000); }); break; }
    case 'aiAccept': { const t=S.txns.find(x=>x.id===+el.dataset.id); if(t&&t.aiSug&&t.aiSug.flat&&t.aiSug.conf>=0.7){ t.flat=t.aiSug.flat; t.kind='instalment'; t.matchBy='AI suggestion, accepted by '+(UI.role||'user')+' ('+Math.round(t.aiSug.conf*100)+'%)'; t.aiSug.accepted=true; recompute(); persistProject(); UI.keepScroll=true; render(); toast('Receipt applied to flat '+t.aiSug.label); } break; }
    case 'aiIgnore': { const t=S.txns.find(x=>x.id===+el.dataset.id); if(t){ delete t.aiSug; persistProject(); UI.keepScroll=true; render(); } break; }
    case 'mgrRefresh': { const ch=syncFromStore(); render(); toast(ch?'Updated with the latest data from the Accountant':(approvalPending()?'Pending approval is shown below':'No new submission found in this browser. The Accountant must submit in the same browser (not Incognito) and see "Submitted and saved".'),6000); break; }
    case 'ping': pingN8n(); break;
    case 'logo': $('#fileLogo').click(); break;
    case 'pdfOne': { const d = demandFor(S, T, el.dataset.flat, UI.demandMs); const L = letterModel(S, T, d, 1, todayDate()); letterPdf(L).save('Demand_Notice_Flat_' + d.label + '.pdf'); break; }
  }
});
document.addEventListener('change', e => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (el.dataset.bind) { const k = el.dataset.bind; if (k === 'flat') UI.flat = el.value; else if (k === 'custFlat') { UI.custFlat = el.value; UI.editSched = false; } else if (k === 'demandMs') { UI.demandMs = +el.value; UI.sel = null; UI.prev = null; } UI.keepScroll = true; render(); return; }
  if (el.dataset.sch) { const m = S.schedule[+el.dataset.i]; const k = el.dataset.sch; if (k === 'pct') m.pct = (parseFloat(el.value) || 0) / 100; else if (k === 'ach') { m.achieved = el.checked; if (el.checked && !m.date) m.date = todayDate(); } else if (k === 'date') m.date = toDate(el.value); else if (k === 'dem') m.demandRaised = el.checked; S.custSchedules.forEach((v, key) => { }); recompute(); UI.keepScroll = true; render(); return; }
  if (el.dataset.tx) { const t = S.txns.find(x => x.id === +el.dataset.id); const k = el.dataset.tx; if (k === 'flat') { t.flat = el.value || null; if (t.flat && (t.kind === 'unmatched')) t.kind = 'instalment'; if (!t.flat && t.kind !== 'unmatched' && t.cr > 0) t.kind = 'unmatched'; } else if (k === 'kind') t.kind = el.value; else if (k === 'cat') { const v = el.value; if (v.startsWith('Taxes')) { t.kind = 'tax_paid'; t.cat = v; } else if (v.startsWith('Inter')) { t.kind = 'transfer_free'; t.cat = v; } else if (v === 'Uncategorised') { t.kind = 'other_payment'; t.cat = v; } else { t.kind = 'cost'; t.cat = v; } } recompute(); UI.keepScroll = true; render(); return; }
  if (el.dataset.pickall) { const all=T.list.filter(f=>f.sold).map(f=>demandFor(S,T,f.norm,UI.demandMs)).filter(Boolean).map(d=>d.flat); UI.sel=el.checked?new Set(all):new Set(); UI.keepScroll=true; render(); return; }
  if (el.dataset.pick) { if (el.checked) UI.sel.add(el.dataset.pick); else UI.sel.delete(el.dataset.pick); UI.keepScroll = true; render(); return; }
  if (el.dataset.info) { S.info[el.dataset.info] = el.value; return; }
  if (el.dataset.set) { const k = el.dataset.set; SET[k] = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : el.value.trim(); saveSet(); UI.keepScroll = true; render(); return; }
  if (el.id === 'fileXlsx' && el.files[0]) loadXlsxFile(el.files[0]);
  if (el.id === 'fileStmt' && el.files.length) { handleFiles(el.files); el.value = ''; }
  if (el.id === 'fileLogo' && el.files[0]) { const rd = new FileReader(); rd.onload = () => { S.logo = rd.result; render(); toast('Logo added to the letterhead'); }; rd.readAsDataURL(el.files[0]); el.value = ''; }
});
document.addEventListener('input', e => { if (e.target.id === 'q') { UI.q = e.target.value; const pos = e.target.selectionStart; UI.keepScroll = true; render(); const q = $('#q'); if (q) { q.focus(); q.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } } });
const tip = () => $('#tip');
document.addEventListener('mouseover', e => {
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-tip]'); if (!el) { tip().classList.remove('on'); return; } const f = T.flats.get(el.dataset.tip); if (!f) { tip().classList.remove('on'); return; } let h;
  if (!f.sold) h = f.unit && f.unit.saleable ? '<b>Unit ' + esc(f.unit.no) + '</b><div><span>Status</span><span>Unsold inventory</span></div><div><span>Area</span><span>' + inr(f.area) + ' sq ft</span></div>' : '<b>Unit ' + esc(f.unit.no) + '</b><div><span>Refuge area, not saleable</span></div>';
  else h = '<b>Flat ' + esc(flatLabel(f.norm)) + ' · ' + esc(f.sale.customer) + '</b><div><span>Area</span><span>' + inr(f.area) + ' sq ft</span></div>' + (f.av > 0 ? '<div><span>Rate</span><span>' + rs(f.rate) + '/sq ft</span></div><div><span>Agreement value</span><span>' + cr(f.av) + '</span></div><div><span>Outstanding</span><span>' + cr(f.out) + ' (' + pct1(f.pctOut) + ')</span></div>' : '<div><span>Sale value not booked</span><span>' + cr(f.recv) + ' recd</span></div>') + (f.flags.length ? '<div style="color:#ffb4ae"><span>' + f.flags.length + ' flag(s)</span><span>' + esc(f.flags[0].title.slice(0, 34)) + '</span></div>' : '');
  const t = tip(); t.innerHTML = h; t.classList.add('on');
});
document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => { const t = tip(); if (!t.classList.contains('on')) return; const x = Math.min(e.clientX + 16, window.innerWidth - 290), y = Math.min(e.clientY + 16, window.innerHeight - 140); t.style.left = x + 'px'; t.style.top = y + 'px'; });
document.addEventListener('mouseout', e => { if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-tip]')) tip().classList.remove('on'); });
const hideTip = () => { const t = tip(); if (t) t.classList.remove('on'); };
['scroll','click','keydown','wheel','touchstart','blur'].forEach(ev => window.addEventListener(ev, hideTip, true));
document.addEventListener('mouseleave', hideTip);
