function workButton(){
  const x=flow();
  if(x.completed)return '<button class="btn" disabled>Milestone confirmed</button>';
  if(!certAccepted())return '<button class="btn" disabled>Upload a certificate that matches the schedule</button>';
  if(!aiGateOk())return '<button class="btn" disabled>Run AI cross-check to unlock</button>';
  if(approvalPending())return '<button class="btn" disabled>Awaiting Manager approval</button>';
  if(approvalOk())return '<button class="btn" data-act="completeFlow">Prepare demands (approved)</button>';
  return '<button class="btn" data-act="apprSubmit">Submit for Manager approval</button>';
}
function approvalBanner(){
  const a=flow().approval;if(!a)return '';
  const when=t=>fmtDate(new Date(t));
  if(a.status==='pending')return '<div class="note s12 info" style="margin-bottom:14px;grid-column:1/-1"><b>Submitted for Manager approval</b> by '+esc(a.submittedBy)+' on '+when(a.submittedAt)+'. Raise Demand and emails unlock after approval.</div>';
  if(a.status==='approved')return '<div class="note s12 ok" style="margin-bottom:14px;grid-column:1/-1"><b>Approved by '+esc(a.approvedBy)+'</b> on '+when(a.approvedAt)+(a.remark?' · '+esc(a.remark):'')+'.</div>';
  return '<div class="note s12 bad" style="margin-bottom:14px;grid-column:1/-1"><b>Returned by '+esc(a.approvedBy)+'</b> on '+when(a.approvedAt)+': '+esc(a.remark||'')+'. Correct and submit again.</div>';
}
function approvalReviewCard(){
  const x=flow();if(!S.loaded||!approvalPending())return '';
  const a=x.approval,c=x.cert||{},F=c.fields||{},A=c.ai&&!c.ai.error?c.ai:null,m=S.schedule.find(q=>q.n===+x.milestone);
  const dem=T.sold.filter(f=>f.av>0);
  const prin=dem.reduce((t,f)=>t+Math.round(f.av*(m?m.pct:0)*100)/100,0),tot=prin*(1+cfg(S).gstRate);
  const row=(l,v,w)=>'<tr><td>'+l+'</td><td class="r">'+v+'</td><td class="r">'+(w==null?'':w)+'</td></tr>';
  const keys=[['milestone','Milestone no.'],['stagePct','Stage %'],['cumulativePct','Cumulative %'],['date','Completion date']];
  const fv=(o,k)=>o[k]==null?'—':k==='date'?fmtDate(toDate(o[k])):esc(String(o[k]));
  let h='<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px;border-left:4px solid #bf9635"><h3>Pending your approval · architect certificate</h3><div class="cert-note">Prepared by '+esc(a.submittedBy)+' on '+fmtDate(new Date(a.submittedAt))+'. Approve only if the certificate supports this stage. Approval unlocks demand raising and demand emails.</div>';
  h+='<table class="mini"><tr><td>Stage</td><td class="r">'+(m?m.n+'. '+esc(m.name):'—')+'</td></tr><tr><td>Demand triggered</td><td class="r">'+(m?pct1(m.pct*100)+' (cumulative '+pct1(cumPctTo(m.n))+')':'—')+'</td></tr><tr><td>Customers · principal · incl. GST</td><td class="r">'+dem.length+' · '+cr(prin)+' · '+cr(tot)+'</td></tr><tr><td>Certificate</td><td class="r">'+esc(F.certificateRef||'—')+' · '+esc(F.architect||'—')+'</td></tr></table>';
  h+='<div class="tw" style="margin-top:10px"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Field</th><th class="r">Rule-based reading</th><th class="r">AI reading</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+keys.map(([k,l])=>row(l,fv(F,k),A?fv(A,k)+' '+certAgree(F,A,k):'<span style="color:#98a2b3">AI not available</span>')).join('')+'</tbody></table></div>';
  if(c.ai&&c.ai.bypass)h+='<p class="cert-note" style="color:#9a6b00">AI service was unavailable; '+esc(c.ai.bypassBy||'')+' proceeded on the rule-based reading.</p>';
  if(c.issues&&c.issues.length)h+='<p class="cert-note" style="color:#b42318">Exceptions: '+c.issues.map(esc).join(' · ')+'</p>';
  if(A&&A.issues&&A.issues.length)h+='<p class="cert-note" style="color:#9a6b00">AI reading raised: '+A.issues.map(esc).join(' · ')+'</p>';
  h+='<div class="tools" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn line" data-act="viewCert">View certificate (PDF)</button><label class="field" style="flex-direction:row;align-items:center;gap:8px">Remark<input id="apprRemark" style="width:280px" placeholder="Optional for approval, required to return"></label><button class="btn" data-act="apprApprove">Approve</button><button class="btn line" data-act="apprReturn">Return to Accountant</button></div></div>';
  return h;
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const el=e.target.closest('[data-act]');if(!el)return;const a=el.dataset.act,x=flow();
  if(a==='confirmRecv'){const d=S.demands.find(q=>q.flat===el.dataset.flat&&q.milestone===+x.milestone);if(d){d.receivedOn=new Date().toISOString();d.receivedBy=UI.user.name;d.status='Received · confirmed by '+UI.user.name+' on '+fmtDate(new Date());persistProject();render();toast('Marked as received');}}
  if(a==='certRemove'){if(x.completed){toast('Demands are already prepared. Use Reset project to start over.',4500);return;}
    if(!confirm('Delete the uploaded certificate "'+((x.certificate||{}).name||'')+'"? You can then upload the correct one.'))return;
    x.certificate=null;x.cert=null;x.approval=null;x.askEmail=false;persistProject();render();toast('Certificate removed. Upload the correct architect certificate.',4000);return;}
  if(a==='apprSubmit'){if(!x.reviewer||!x.reviewer.trim()){toast('Enter the reviewer (preparer) name first');return;}
    if(!certAccepted()){toast('Certificate does not match the payment schedule. Resolve the exceptions or accept after manual review.',4500);return;}
    if(!aiGateOk()){toast('Run the AI cross-check first');return;}
    x.approval={status:'pending',submittedBy:x.reviewer.trim(),submittedAt:new Date().toISOString()};const saved=persistProject();render();toast(saved?'Submitted and saved. The Manager will see it under Demand Schedule (Switch user, or open the tool in the same browser).':'Submitted here, but it could NOT be saved for the Manager because browser storage is full. Use a smaller certificate PDF or click Backup now.',saved?5000:9000);}
  if(a==='apprApprove'&&approvalPending()){x.approval=Object.assign(x.approval,{status:'approved',approvedBy:UI.user.name,approvedAt:new Date().toISOString(),remark:(($('#apprRemark')||{}).value||'').trim()});persistProject();render();toast('Approved. The Accountant can now prepare and send the demands.',4000);}
  if(a==='apprReturn'&&approvalPending()){const r=(($('#apprRemark')||{}).value||'').trim();if(!r){toast('Enter a remark to return it');return;}
    x.approval=Object.assign(x.approval,{status:'returned',approvedBy:UI.user.name,approvedAt:new Date().toISOString(),remark:r});persistProject();render();toast('Returned to the Accountant.',4000);}
  if(a==='viewCert'){const d=x.certificate&&x.certificate.data;if(!d){toast('The certificate PDF was too large to share through this browser. Ask the Accountant to send the PDF; the extracted readings below are still available for your review.',7000);return;}
    try{const bytes=typeof d==='string'?b64ToBytes(d.replace(/^data:[^,]*,/,'')):new Uint8Array(d);window.open(URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([bytes],{type:'application/pdf'})),'_blank');}catch(err){toast('Could not open the certificate');}}
});

function demandSummaryCard(){
  if(!S.loaded)return '';
  const done=S.schedule.filter(m=>m.demandRaised||S.demands.some(d=>d.milestone===m.n&&(d.issued||d.ok))),m=done[done.length-1];
  let h='<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px"><h3>Demand status · summary</h3>';
  if(!m)return h+'<div class="note info">No stage demand has been raised yet.</div></div>';
  const ds=S.demands.filter(d=>d.milestone===m.n&&(d.issued||d.ok)),elig=T.sold.filter(f=>f.av>0).length;
  if(!ds.length)return h+'<div class="note ok"><b>'+pct1(m.pct*100)+' demand (cumulative '+pct1(cumPctTo(m.n))+') raised on all customers as per the master data.</b> Stage '+m.n+': '+esc(m.name)+(m.date?', completed '+fmtDate(m.date):'')+'. The next stage is raised through the Accountant\'s Demand Workflow after your approval.</div></div>';
  const on=ds.map(d=>d.issuedOn||'').filter(Boolean).sort().pop();
  const dt=on?fmtDate(new Date(on+'T00:00:00Z')):(ds[0]?fmtDate(ds[0].date):'—');
  const prin=ds.reduce((a,d)=>a+(d.principal||0),0),tot=ds.reduce((a,d)=>a+(d.amount||0),0),got=ds.reduce((a,d)=>a+receiptForDemand(d).paid,0),mailed=ds.filter(d=>d.ok).length;
  h+='<div class="note ok"><b>'+pct1(m.pct*100)+' demand (cumulative '+pct1(cumPctTo(m.n))+') has been raised on '+(ds.length===elig?'all '+elig:ds.length+' of '+elig)+' customers on '+dt+'.</b> Stage '+m.n+': '+esc(m.name)+'.</div>';
  h+='<table class="mini" style="margin-top:10px"><tr><td>Principal demanded</td><td class="r">'+cr(prin)+'</td></tr><tr><td>Total demand incl. GST</td><td class="r">'+cr(tot)+'</td></tr><tr><td>Demand emails sent</td><td class="r">'+mailed+' of '+ds.length+'</td></tr><tr><td>Received against this demand</td><td class="r">'+cr(got)+'</td></tr><tr><td>Balance to collect</td><td class="r">'+cr(Math.max(0,tot-got))+'</td></tr>'+(flow().approval&&flow().approval.status==='approved'?'<tr><td>Certificate approved by</td><td class="r">'+esc(flow().approval.approvedBy)+' · '+fmtDate(new Date(flow().approval.approvedAt))+'</td></tr>':'')+'</table></div>';
  return h;
}

function currentDemandCard(){
  if(!S.loaded)return '';
  const x=flow(),m=S.schedule.find(q=>q.n===+x.milestone),ds=S.demands.filter(d=>d.milestone===+x.milestone);
  const done=baseProgress(),nxt=(m&&!m.achieved)?cumPctTo(m.n):null,last=[...S.schedule].filter(q=>q.achieved).pop();
  const st=x.completed?'Milestone confirmed · raise demands and send emails':approvalOk()?'Approved by Manager · prepare demands':approvalPending()?'Awaiting Manager approval':(x.certificate&&x.cert&&!certAccepted())?'Certificate rejected · upload the correct certificate or accept after manual review':aiGateOk()?'AI cross-check done · submit for Manager approval':(x.certificate&&certAccepted())?'Certificate read · run the AI cross-check':x.certificate?'Certificate not recognised · review':'Awaiting architect certificate';
  const bar='<div style="height:14px;background:#e8ecf1;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;display:flex;margin:6px 0 4px"><div style="width:'+done+'%;background:var(--brass)"></div>'+(nxt&&nxt>done?'<div style="width:'+(nxt-done)+'%;background:repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,#f0d58a 0 5px,#f8e9bd 5px 10px)"></div>':'')+'</div>';
  return '<div class="card s12" style="margin-bottom:14px"><h3>Current demand</h3>'+bar+'<div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--muted)">'+pct1(done)+' certified'+(nxt?' · next stage adds '+pct1(nxt-done)+' → '+pct1(nxt)+' once the certificate is approved':'')+'</div><table class="mini" style="margin-top:8px"><tr><td>Work certified so far</td><td class="r">'+pct1(done)+(last?' · Stage '+last.n+': '+esc(last.name):'')+'</td></tr><tr><td>'+(m&&m.achieved?'Current demand':'Next demand')+'</td><td class="r">'+(m?'Stage '+m.n+': '+esc(m.name)+' · '+pct1(m.pct*100)+' (cumulative '+pct1(cumPctTo(m.n))+')':'—')+'</td></tr><tr><td>Status</td><td class="r"><b>'+st+'</b></td></tr><tr><td>Demands prepared · raised · emails sent</td><td class="r">'+ds.length+' · '+ds.filter(d=>d.issued||d.ok).length+' · '+ds.filter(d=>d.ok).length+'</td></tr></table></div>';

function remindedCard(){
  if(!S.loaded)return '';
  const rem=(S.reminders||[]),flats=[...new Set(rem.flatMap(r=>r.flats))],list=(()=>{const a=flats.map(n=>T.flats.get(n)).filter(Boolean),seen=new Set(a.map(f=>f.norm));overdueFlats().forEach(f=>{if(!seen.has(f.norm))a.push(f);});return a;})();
  let h='<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px"><h3>Customers reminded · outstanding position</h3>';
  if(!list.length)return h+'<div class="note info">No reminder has been sent yet. Send overdue reminders first (Overdue Reminders tab). Outstanding figures update here when the bank statement is uploaded.</div></div>';
  const snap=n=>{for(let i=rem.length-1;i>=0;i--){if(rem[i].snap&&rem[i].snap[n]!=null)return rem[i];}return null;};
  h+='<div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Flat</th><th>Customer</th><th>Reminder sent</th><th class="r">Overdue when reminded ₹</th><th class="r">Overdue now ₹</th><th class="r">Total outstanding now ₹</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+list.map(f=>{const r=snap(f.norm);const o=f.overdue||0;return '<tr><td><b>'+esc(f.sale.label)+'</b></td><td>'+esc(f.sale.customer)+'</td><td>'+(r?fmtDate(r.date)+(/Dry/.test(r.status)?' (test run)':''):'—')+'</td><td class="r">'+(r?inr(r.snap[f.norm]):'—')+'</td><td class="r">'+(o>0.5?inr(o):'—')+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(f.out)+'</td><td>'+(o>0.5?pill('amber',r?'Still overdue':'Not yet reminded'):pill('ok','Overdue cleared'))+'</td></tr>';}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div><div class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">Updates automatically when a bank statement is uploaded below. Project outstanding: '+cr(T.out)+' · overdue '+cr(T.overdue)+'.</div></div>';
  return h;
}
function emailStatusCard(){
  const x=flow(),ds=S.demands.filter(d=>d.milestone===+x.milestone);
  if(!ds.length)return '';
  return '<div class="card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>Demand email status <small>'+ds.length+' demands · current milestone</small></h3><div class="tw"><table class="t"><thead><tr><th>Flat</th><th>Customer</th><th class="r">Demand ₹</th><th>Email status</th><th>Receipt confirmation</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+ds.map(d=>{const acc=d.ok&&/accepted|^Email sent/i.test(d.status||'')&&!d.receivedOn;return '<tr><td><b>'+esc(d.label)+'</b></td><td>'+esc((S.sales.get(d.flat)||{}).customer||'')+'</td><td class="r">'+inr(d.amount)+'</td><td>'+(d.ok?pill('ok',esc(d.status||'Sent')):pill('grey',esc(d.status||'Not sent')))+'</td><td>'+(acc?'<button class="btn line sm" data-act="confirmRecv" data-flat="'+esc(d.flat)+'">Mark as received</button>':d.receivedOn?'<span style="font-size:12px;color:#067647">Received · confirmed by '+esc(d.receivedBy||'')+'</span>':'&mdash;')+'</td></tr>';}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div><p class="cert-note" style="margin-top:8px">Email sent means it was handed to the mail service. If the customer or test inbox confirms it arrived, click Mark as received; the name and time are recorded and flow into the Demand Register.</p></div>';
}
function vDemailFive(){
  const x=flow();
  if(!S.loaded)return '';
  if(!x.completed)return '<div class="card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>Raise demand</h3><p>Available after the architect certificate is uploaded and the milestone is confirmed.</p></div>';

}
function regRows(){
  return [['Reference','Flat','Customer','Date','Milestone','Principal Rs','GST Rs','Total demand Rs','Received Rs','Balance Rs','Email status'],
    ...S.demands.map(d=>{const r=receiptForDemand(d);return [d.ref,d.label,(S.sales.get(d.flat)||{}).customer||'',fmtDate(d.date),(S.schedule[d.milestone-1]||{}).name||'',+(d.principal||0).toFixed(2),+(d.gst||0).toFixed(2),+(d.amount||0).toFixed(2),+(r.paid||0).toFixed(2),+(r.remaining||0).toFixed(2),d.status||''];})];
}
const REGS={
  demand:{title:'Demand Register',file:'Demand_Register',empty:'No demands raised yet.'},
  receipt:{title:'Receipt Register',file:'Receipt_Register',empty:'No bank statement uploaded yet.'},
  reminder:{title:'Overdue Reminder Register',file:'Overdue_Reminder_Register',empty:'No overdue reminder sent yet.'}
};
function regRowsFor(k){
  if(k==='demand')return regRows();
  if(k==='receipt'){
    const kinds=Object.fromEntries(CREDIT_KINDS);
    return [['Date','Bank narration','Receipt Rs','Receipt type','Flat','Customer','Matched by','Demand reference','Status','Statement file'],
      ...S.txns.filter(t=>t.cr>0).map(t=>{const sa=t.flat?S.sales.get(t.flat):null;return [fmtDate(t.date),t.narr||'',+(t.cr||0).toFixed(2),kinds[t.kind]||t.kind||'',t.flat?((T.flats.get(t.flat)||{}).sale||{}).label||t.flat:'',(sa||{}).customer||'',t.matchBy||'',t.demRef||((S.demands.find(d=>receiptForDemand(d).matches.includes(t))||{}).ref)||'',t.flat?'Matched':'Unmatched',t.file||''];})];
  }
  const rows=[];(S.reminders||[]).forEach(r=>{r.flats.forEach(n=>{const f=T.flats.get(n);const sa=S.sales.get(n)||{};rows.push([fmtDate(r.date),f?f.sale.label:n,sa.customer||'',(S.customers.get(n)||{}).email||'',+((r.snap||{})[n]||0).toFixed(2),f?+(f.overdue||0).toFixed(2):0,f&&(f.overdue||0)>0.5?'Still overdue':'Overdue cleared',r.status||'',r.batchId||'']);});});
  return [['Reminder date','Flat','Customer','Email','Overdue when reminded Rs','Overdue now Rs','Position now','Email status','Batch'],...rows];
}
function regCard(k){
  const R=REGS[k],rows=regRowsFor(k),n=rows.length-1,H=rows[0];
  const numCol=H.map((h,c)=>rows.length>1&&typeof rows[1][c]==='number');
  return '<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px"><h3>'+R.title+' <small>'+n+' entr'+(n===1?'y':'ies')+'</small></h3>'+(n?'<div class="tw" style="max-height:300px"><table class="t"><thead><tr>'+H.map((h,c)=>'<th'+(numCol[c]?' class="r"':'')+'>'+esc(h)+'</th>').join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.slice(1).reverse().slice(0,200).map(r=>'<tr>'+r.map((v,c)=>'<td'+(numCol[c]?' class="r"':'')+'>'+(numCol[c]?inr(v):esc(String(v)))+'</td>').join('')+'</tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'+(n>200?'<div class="hint">Showing the latest 200. The download has all '+n+'.</div>':''):'<div class="empty">'+R.empty+'</div>')
   +'<div class="tools" style="margin-top:12px"><label class="field" style="flex-direction:row;align-items:center;gap:8px">Download as <select id="regFmt_'+k+'"><option value="xlsx">Excel</option><option value="pdf">PDF</option><option value="csv">CSV</option></select></label><button class="btn" data-act="regDownload" data-reg="'+k+'"'+(n?'':' disabled')+'>Download</button></div></div>';
}
function vReports(){return '<h2 class="pg">Reports</h2><p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:12px">One register for each Accountant task: demands raised, receipts from the bank statements, and overdue reminders sent.</p>'+['demand','receipt','reminder'].map(regCard).join('');}
function downloadRegister(fmt,k){
  k=k||'demand';const R=REGS[k],rows=regRowsFor(k),name=R.file,sn=R.title.slice(0,31);
  if(fmt==='xlsx'){const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new(),ws=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rows);ws['!cols']=rows[0].map((h,c)=>({wch:Math.min(48,Math.max(String(h).length,...rows.slice(1,60).map(r=>String(r[c]??'').length))+2)}));XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,sn);XLSX.writeFile(wb,name+'.xlsx');return;}
  if(fmt==='csv'){const csv=rows.map(r=>r.map(v=>'"'+String(v).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"').join(',')).join('\r\n');const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}));a.download=name+'.csv';a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);return;}
  const {jsPDF}=window.jspdf,doc=new jsPDF({unit:'mm',format:'a4',orientation:'landscape'});
  const safe=v=>String(v??'').replace(/₹/g,'Rs. ').replace(/[–—·]/g,'-').replace(/[^\x20-\x7e]/g,'');
  const H=rows[0],num=H.map((h,c)=>rows.length>1&&typeof rows[1][c]==='number');
  const want=H.map((h,c)=>Math.min(60,Math.max(String(h).length,...rows.slice(1,80).map(r=>String(num[c]?inr(r[c]):r[c]??'').length),6)));const tw=want.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),W=want.map(w=>w/tw*277);
  let y=16,pg=1;
  const head=()=>{doc.setFont('helvetica','bold');doc.setFontSize(13);doc.text(R.title+' - '+safe(infoGet(S,'Project Name','')),10,y);doc.setFontSize(8);doc.setFont('helvetica','normal');doc.text('As on '+fmtDate(T.ctx.asOf)+'  |  '+(rows.length-1)+' entries  |  Demonstration data',10,y+5);y+=11;doc.setFont('helvetica','bold');doc.setFontSize(7);let x=10;H.forEach((h,c)=>{doc.text(safe(h),num[c]?x+W[c]-1.5:x,y,{align:num[c]?'right':'left'});x+=W[c];});y+=2;doc.line(10,y,287,y);y+=4;doc.setFont('helvetica','normal');};
  head();
  rows.slice(1).forEach(r=>{if(y>192){doc.setFontSize(7);doc.text('Page '+pg,280,205);doc.addPage();pg++;y=16;head();}let x=10;doc.setFontSize(7);r.forEach((v,c)=>{const t=doc.splitTextToSize(num[c]?safe(inr(v)):safe(v),W[c]-2)[0]||'';doc.text(t,num[c]?x+W[c]-1.5:x,y,{align:num[c]?'right':'left'});x+=W[c];});y+=5.5;});
  doc.setFontSize(7);doc.text('Page '+pg,280,205);doc.save(name+'.pdf');
}

See the demand letter for bank details.';
 window.open('https://wa.me/'+number+'?text='+encodeURIComponent(message),'_blank','noopener,noreferrer');
}

 const summary=[['Metric','Value','Basis'],['Project',infoGet(S,'Project Name',''),''],['As on',fmtDate(T.ctx.asOf),''],['Booked units',T.soldN,'Includes accounts with missing AV'],['Unsold units',T.unsoldN,''],['Sold area sq ft',T.areaSold,''],['Total saleable area sq ft',T.areaTotal,''],['Sold area %',p.areaPct/100,'Sold area / saleable area'],['Average rate Rs / sq ft',T.rateAvg,'Recorded AV / corresponding area'],['Recorded agreement value Rs',T.av,'Excludes missing AV'],['Principal collected Rs',T.matchedRecv,'Only accounts with AV; GST excluded'],['Outstanding principal Rs',T.out,'Recorded AV less matched collections'],['Reconciliation difference Rs',{t:'n',f:'B10-B11-B12',v:0},'Must be zero'],['Separate advances Rs',T.unallocatedRecv,'Missing agreement value; excluded above'],['Work completed %',p.work/100,'Certificate demo status; review remains separate'],['Demand raised principal Rs',p.raisedAmount,'Drafts excluded'],['Demand raised %',p.demandPct/100,'Raised principal / recorded AV'],['Principal collected %',p.collectionPct/100,'Matched principal / recorded AV'],['Cost budget Rs',T.costBudget,'Excel input'],['Cost incurred Rs',T.costIncurred,'Excel input'],['Budget remaining Rs',{t:'n',f:'B19-B20',v:T.costBudget-T.costIncurred},'Budget less incurred'],['Estimated sale Rs',T.estimatedSale,'Unsold + missing AV estimate'],['Total projected revenue Rs',{t:'n',f:'B10+B22',v:T.projectedRevenue},'Recorded AV + estimates'],['GST rate (demo)',cfg(S).gstRate,'Non-affordable residential booked before completion / first occupation'],['Missing AV accounts',T.sold.filter(f=>f.av<=0).length,'Enter AV before demand generation'],['Registration','Synthetic demo details','Not a legal verification'],['Stamp duty / registration','Illustrative sample amounts','Not statutory rate advice']];
 const main=sheet('Dashboard',summary,[36,34,75]);[8,15,17,18,24].forEach(r=>main['B'+r].z='0.00%');[4,5,6,7,25].forEach(r=>main['B'+r].z='#,##0');
 const cs=sheet('Customer Accounts',[['Flat','Customer','Area sq ft','Agreement value Rs','Principal received Rs','Outstanding Rs','Separate advance Rs','GST rate','GST billed Rs','GST received Rs','GST balance Rs','Stamp duty + registration Rs','Received duty + registration Rs','Registered (demo)','Registration ref (synthetic)','Email','Mobile placeholder','AV status'],...T.sold.map(f=>[f.sale.label,f.sale.customer,f.area,f.av>0?f.av:null,f.av>0?f.recv:null,f.av>0?f.out:null,f.av>0?null:f.recv,cfg(S).gstRate,f.av>0?f.gst:null,f.gstRecd,f.av>0?f.gstBal:null,f.sd,f.sdRecd,f.cust.registered?'Y':'N',f.cust.regNo||'',f.cust.email||'',f.cust.mobile||'',f.av>0?'Recorded':'Required before demand'])],[10,22,14,23,23,23,23,12,22,22,22,28,28,18,27,40,22,27]);
 for(let r=2;r<=T.sold.length+1;r++)cs['H'+r].z='0%';
 sheet('Demand Register',[['Reference','Flat','Customer','Date','Principal Rs','GST Rs','Rounding Rs','Gross demand Rs','Matched bank receipt Rs','Demand balance Rs','Raised','Email status','Demo email note'],...S.demands.map(d=>[d.ref,d.label,(S.sales.get(d.flat)||{}).customer||'',fmtDate(d.date),d.principal,d.gst,d.rounding||0,d.amount,receiptForDemand(d).paid,receiptForDemand(d).remaining,d.issued||d.ok?'Y':'N',d.status,(S.emailBatches||[]).filter(b=>b.milestone===d.milestone).map(b=>b.note).join(' | ')])],[24,10,22,17,22,22,18,22,25,22,12,43,85]);
 sheet('Cost Budget',[['Cost head','Budget Rs','Incurred Rs','Remaining Rs'],...S.cost.map(r=>[r.head,r.budget,r.incurred,r.budget-r.incurred])],[45,25,25,25]);
 upgradeSheets(sheet);
 return wb;
}

function managementReportLines(){
 const cost=S.cost.reduce((a,r)=>({budget:a.budget+r.budget,incurred:a.incurred+r.incurred}),{budget:0,incurred:0});
 const reg=[...S.customers.values()].filter(c=>c.registered&&S.sales.has(c.norm)).length;
 const ds=S.demands,paid=ds.reduce((a,d)=>a+receiptForDemand(d).paid,0);
 const money=n=>'Rs. '+Number(n||0).toLocaleString('en-IN',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2});
 return ['As on: '+fmtDate(T.ctx.asOf),'Inventory: '+T.saleable+' flats | Booked: '+T.soldN+' | Unsold: '+T.unsoldN,'Registered (accounting classification): '+reg+' | Registration pending: '+(T.soldN-reg),'Reported physical work completed: '+physicalProgress()+'%'+(flow().certificate&&!flow().completed?' (review pending)':''),'Agreement value: '+money(T.av),'Principal collections (with agreement value): '+money(T.matchedRecv),'Separate advances (agreement value missing): '+money(T.unallocatedRecv),'Net principal balance: '+money(T.out),'Project budget: '+money(cost.budget),'Cost incurred: '+money(cost.incurred),'Remaining budget: '+money(cost.budget-cost.incurred),'Demands prepared: '+ds.length+' | Gross value: '+money(ds.reduce((a,d)=>a+d.amount,0)),'Demand-linked receipts: '+money(paid),'Unpaid demand balance: '+money(ds.reduce((a,d)=>a+receiptForDemand(d).remaining,0))];
}
